"""
mesh/daemon.py — Background daemon, authentication token manager, and OS login auto-start manager.

Addresses Findings:
  - S9: Tracks `(st_mtime_ns, st_size)` of `~/.mesh/auth_token` so token rotations on disk
        are picked up immediately by running processes without a restart, and adds
        `rotate_auth_token()` + `mesh daemon rotate-token` CLI action.
  - S9: Supports optional Unix domain socket (`MESH_UNIX_SOCKET`) when spawning the daemon.
  - N5 & N6: Rejects symlinks (`reject_symlink`) and applies Windows owner-only ACLs via
             `secure_file_permissions` on `auth_token`, `daemon.pid`, `daemon.log`, and `.autostart_registered`.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import hmac
import http.client
import json
import os
import re
import secrets
import shlex
import signal
import socket
import stat
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Optional, Union
from xml.sax.saxutils import escape as xml_escape

from .audit import (
    ensure_secure_mesh_dir,
    get_mesh_dir,
    reject_symlink,
    secure_file_permissions,
)
from .envutil import env_float, env_int
from .store import _acquire_os_file_lock, _release_os_file_lock


def _pid_file() -> Path:
    return get_mesh_dir() / "daemon.pid"


def _log_file() -> Path:
    return get_mesh_dir() / "daemon.log"


def _auth_token_file() -> Path:
    return get_mesh_dir() / "auth_token"


def _autostart_marker_file() -> Path:
    return get_mesh_dir() / ".autostart_registered"


DAEMON_PID_FILE = _pid_file()
DAEMON_LOG_FILE = _log_file()
AUTH_TOKEN_FILE = _auth_token_file()
AUTOSTART_MARKER_FILE = _autostart_marker_file()

DEFAULT_PORT = env_int("MESH_HTTP_PORT", 7701, minimum=1, maximum=65535)
DEFAULT_HOST = os.environ.get("MESH_HTTP_HOST", "127.0.0.1")
DEFAULT_IDLE_TIMEOUT = env_float("MESH_IDLE_UNLOAD_SECONDS", 180.0, minimum=0.0)

_WIN_RUN_KEY = r"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run"
_WIN_RUN_VALUE_NAME = "MeshContextDaemon"

_SPAWN_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_LAST_SPAWN_TS = 0.0
_TOKEN_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_CACHED_TOKEN: Optional[str] = None
_CACHED_TOKEN_PATH: Optional[Path] = None
_CACHED_TOKEN_STAMP: Optional[tuple[int, int]] = None

_DESKTOP_EXEC_SPECIAL_RE = re.compile(r'[\s"\'`$\\><~|&;*?#()]')


def _format_desktop_exec_arg(arg: str) -> str:
    s = str(arg).replace("%", "%%")
    if not s:
        return '""'
    if not _DESKTOP_EXEC_SPECIAL_RE.search(s):
        return s
    escaped = (
        s.replace("\\", "\\\\")
        .replace('"', '\\"')
        .replace("`", "\\`")
        .replace("$", "\\$")
    )
    return f'"{escaped}"'


def _format_desktop_exec_command(args: list[str]) -> str:
    return " ".join(_format_desktop_exec_arg(str(a)) for a in args)


def _get_token_file_stamp(token_file: Path) -> Optional[tuple[int, int]]:
    try:
        if not token_file.exists():
            return None
        st = token_file.stat()
        return (int(st.st_mtime_ns), int(st.st_size))
    except OSError:
        return None


def _safe_fchmod(fd: int, mode: int) -> None:
    fchmod = getattr(os, "fchmod", None)
    if fchmod is not None:
        try:
            fchmod(fd, mode)
        except (AttributeError, OSError):
            pass


def _write_private_file(path: Path, content: str) -> None:
    """
    Write `content` to `path` creating the file with 0600 permissions atomically from birth
    (and owner-only Windows ACLs via `secure_file_permissions`, Findings N5, N6).
    """
    ensure_secure_mesh_dir(path.parent)
    reject_symlink(path)
    tmp_path = path.with_suffix(f".tmp.{os.getpid()}.{threading.get_ident()}")
    reject_symlink(tmp_path)
    if os.name == "nt":
        tmp_path.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
        os.replace(tmp_path, path)
        secure_file_permissions(path)
        return

    flags = os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC
    if hasattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW"):
        flags |= os.O_NOFOLLOW
    fd = os.open(str(tmp_path), flags, stat.S_IRUSR | stat.S_IWUSR)
    _safe_fchmod(fd, stat.S_IRUSR | stat.S_IWUSR)
    with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(content)
    secure_file_permissions(tmp_path)
    os.replace(tmp_path, path)
    secure_file_permissions(path)


def _open_private_log_append(path: Path) -> Any:
    """Open `daemon.log` in append mode with 0600 permissions on POSIX and owner ACLs on Windows."""
    ensure_secure_mesh_dir(path.parent)
    reject_symlink(path)
    if os.name == "nt":
        fh = open(path, "a", encoding="utf-8")
        secure_file_permissions(path)
        return fh
    flags = os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_APPEND
    if hasattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW"):
        flags |= os.O_NOFOLLOW
    fd = os.open(str(path), flags, stat.S_IRUSR | stat.S_IWUSR)
    _safe_fchmod(fd, stat.S_IRUSR | stat.S_IWUSR)
    return os.fdopen(fd, "a", encoding="utf-8")


def _read_valid_token_file(token_file: Path) -> Optional[str]:
    reject_symlink(token_file)
    if not token_file.exists():
        return None
    try:
        secure_file_permissions(token_file)
        existing = token_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
        if len(existing) >= 16:
            return existing
    except OSError:
        pass
    return None


def _try_atomic_create_token_file(token_file: Path, token: str) -> bool:
    reject_symlink(token_file)
    flags = os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL
    flags |= getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0)
    mode = stat.S_IRUSR | stat.S_IWUSR if os.name != "nt" else 0o600
    try:
        fd = os.open(str(token_file), flags, mode)
    except FileExistsError:
        return False
    except OSError:
        if token_file.exists():
            return False
        raise
    try:
        if os.name != "nt":
            _safe_fchmod(fd, stat.S_IRUSR | stat.S_IWUSR)
        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(token)
            f.flush()
            try:
                os.fsync(f.fileno())
            except OSError:
                pass
        secure_file_permissions(token_file)
        return True
    except Exception:
        try:
            token_file.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        except OSError:
            pass
        raise


def get_or_create_auth_token() -> str:
    """
    Return the local Mesh authentication token stored in `~/.mesh/auth_token` (mode 0600).
    Finding S9: Revalidates `(st_mtime_ns, st_size)` against `_CACHED_TOKEN_STAMP` so
    `rotate_auth_token()` updates are immediately visible to all local processes.
    """
    global _CACHED_TOKEN, _CACHED_TOKEN_PATH, _CACHED_TOKEN_STAMP
    env_tok = (os.environ.get("MESH_AUTH_TOKEN") or os.environ.get("MESH_TOKEN") or "").strip()
    if env_tok:
        return env_tok
    token_file = _auth_token_file()
    cur_stamp = _get_token_file_stamp(token_file)
    if (
        _CACHED_TOKEN
        and _CACHED_TOKEN_PATH == token_file
        and cur_stamp is not None
        and _CACHED_TOKEN_STAMP == cur_stamp
    ):
        return _CACHED_TOKEN

    with _TOKEN_LOCK:
        token_file = _auth_token_file()
        cur_stamp = _get_token_file_stamp(token_file)
        if (
            _CACHED_TOKEN
            and _CACHED_TOKEN_PATH == token_file
            and cur_stamp is not None
            and _CACHED_TOKEN_STAMP == cur_stamp
        ):
            return _CACHED_TOKEN
        ensure_secure_mesh_dir(token_file.parent)

        existing = _read_valid_token_file(token_file)
        if existing is not None:
            _CACHED_TOKEN = existing
            _CACHED_TOKEN_PATH = token_file
            _CACHED_TOKEN_STAMP = _get_token_file_stamp(token_file)
            return existing

        token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
        try:
            if _try_atomic_create_token_file(token_file, token):
                _CACHED_TOKEN = token
                _CACHED_TOKEN_PATH = token_file
                _CACHED_TOKEN_STAMP = _get_token_file_stamp(token_file)
                return token
        except OSError:
            pass

        for attempt in range(6):
            existing = _read_valid_token_file(token_file)
            if existing is not None:
                _CACHED_TOKEN = existing
                _CACHED_TOKEN_PATH = token_file
                _CACHED_TOKEN_STAMP = _get_token_file_stamp(token_file)
                return existing
            if attempt < 5:
                time.sleep(0.005 * (attempt + 1))

        repair_lock_path = token_file.with_name(".auth_token.lock")
        lock_fd: Optional[int] = None
        try:
            lock_fd = _acquire_os_file_lock(repair_lock_path, timeout=5.0)
            existing = _read_valid_token_file(token_file)
            if existing is not None:
                _CACHED_TOKEN = existing
                _CACHED_TOKEN_PATH = token_file
                _CACHED_TOKEN_STAMP = _get_token_file_stamp(token_file)
                return existing

            try:
                token_file.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            except OSError:
                pass

            try:
                if _try_atomic_create_token_file(token_file, token):
                    _CACHED_TOKEN = token
                    _CACHED_TOKEN_PATH = token_file
                    _CACHED_TOKEN_STAMP = _get_token_file_stamp(token_file)
                    return token
            except OSError:
                pass

            existing = _read_valid_token_file(token_file)
            if existing is not None:
                _CACHED_TOKEN = existing
                _CACHED_TOKEN_PATH = token_file
                _CACHED_TOKEN_STAMP = _get_token_file_stamp(token_file)
                return existing

            _write_private_file(token_file, token)
            persisted = _read_valid_token_file(token_file)
            if persisted is not None:
                token = persisted
        except OSError:
            persisted = _read_valid_token_file(token_file)
            if persisted is not None:
                token = persisted
        finally:
            if lock_fd is not None:
                _release_os_file_lock(lock_fd)

        _CACHED_TOKEN = token
        _CACHED_TOKEN_PATH = token_file
        _CACHED_TOKEN_STAMP = _get_token_file_stamp(token_file)
        return token


def rotate_auth_token() -> str:
    """
    Atomically generate and write a fresh 256-bit authentication token to `~/.mesh/auth_token`,
    invalidating in-memory token and daemon identity caches (Finding S9).
    """
    global _CACHED_TOKEN, _CACHED_TOKEN_PATH, _CACHED_TOKEN_STAMP
    token_file = _auth_token_file()
    ensure_secure_mesh_dir(token_file.parent)
    new_token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
    repair_lock_path = token_file.with_name(".auth_token.lock")
    lock_fd: Optional[int] = None
    with _TOKEN_LOCK:
        try:
            lock_fd = _acquire_os_file_lock(repair_lock_path, timeout=5.0)
            _write_private_file(token_file, new_token)
            _CACHED_TOKEN = new_token
            _CACHED_TOKEN_PATH = token_file
            _CACHED_TOKEN_STAMP = _get_token_file_stamp(token_file)
        finally:
            if lock_fd is not None:
                _release_os_file_lock(lock_fd)
    with _VERIFIED_DAEMON_LOCK:
        _VERIFIED_DAEMON_CACHE.clear()
    return new_token


def get_auth_headers() -> dict[str, str]:
    """Return HTTP headers containing the local Mesh bearer token[cite: 2]."""
    tok = get_or_create_auth_token()
    return {
        "Authorization": f"Bearer {tok}",
        "X-Mesh-Token": tok,
    }


def request_user_permission(action_desc: str, assume_yes: bool = False) -> bool:
    """
    Prompt the user with a `y/n` question before starting a background process,
    downloading model weights, or modifying shell config files[cite: 2].
    """
    try:
        from . import ui

        return ui.confirm(
            f"Allow Mesh to {action_desc}?",
            assume_yes=assume_yes,
        )
    except Exception:
        pass
    if assume_yes or os.environ.get("MESH_AUTO_APPROVE", "").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes"):
        return True
    if os.environ.get("MESH_NON_INTERACTIVE", "").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes"):
        return False
    if not (
        hasattr(sys.stdin, "isatty")
        and sys.stdin.isatty()
        and hasattr(sys.stdout, "isatty")
        and sys.stdout.isatty()
    ):
        return False
    try:
        ans = input(f"[Mesh] Allow Mesh to {action_desc}? (y/n): ").strip().lower()
        return ans in ("y", "yes")
    except (EOFError, KeyboardInterrupt, OSError):
        print()
        return False


def _health_url(host: str = DEFAULT_HOST, port: int = DEFAULT_PORT, nonce: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
    base = f"http://{host}:{port}/health"
    if nonce:
        return f"{base}?{urllib.parse.urlencode({'nonce': nonce})}"
    return base


_VERIFIED_DAEMON_CACHE: dict[tuple[str, int], tuple[float, dict[str, Any]]] = {}
_VERIFIED_DAEMON_LOCK = threading.Lock()


def invalidate_daemon_identity_cache(
    host: Optional[str] = DEFAULT_HOST,
    port: Optional[int] = DEFAULT_PORT,
) -> None:
    h = host or DEFAULT_HOST
    p = int(port or DEFAULT_PORT)
    with _VERIFIED_DAEMON_LOCK:
        _VERIFIED_DAEMON_CACHE.pop((h, p), None)


def get_daemon_health(
    host: str = DEFAULT_HOST,
    port: int = DEFAULT_PORT,
    timeout: float = 0.6,
) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
    url = f"http://{host}:{port}/health"
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            url,
            headers={"Accept": "application/json"},
            method="GET",
        )
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:
            if resp.status == 200:
                data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
                if isinstance(data, dict) and data.get("status") == "ok":
                    return data
    except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError, ValueError):
        pass
    return None


def verify_daemon_identity(
    host: str = DEFAULT_HOST,
    port: int = DEFAULT_PORT,
    timeout: float = 0.5,
) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
    key = (host, int(port))
    now = time.monotonic()
    with _VERIFIED_DAEMON_LOCK:
        cached = _VERIFIED_DAEMON_CACHE.get(key)
        if cached is not None:
            expires_at, data = cached
            if now < expires_at:
                return data
            _VERIFIED_DAEMON_CACHE.pop(key, None)

    token = get_or_create_auth_token()
    nonce = secrets.token_hex(32)
    nonce_bytes = bytes.fromhex(nonce)
    expected_proof = hmac.new(token.encode("utf-8"), nonce_bytes, hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()

    conn = http.client.HTTPConnection(host, port, timeout=timeout)
    try:
        conn.connect()
        if conn.sock is not None:
            try:
                conn.sock.setsockopt(socket.IPPROTO_TCP, socket.TCP_NODELAY, 1)
            except Exception:
                pass
        conn.request(
            "GET",
            f"/identity?nonce={nonce}",
            headers={"Accept": "application/json", "X-Mesh-Nonce": nonce},
        )
        resp = conn.getresponse()
        if resp.status == 200:
            body = resp.read().decode("utf-8")
            data = json.loads(body)
            if isinstance(data, dict) and data.get("proof"):
                actual_proof = str(data["proof"]).strip()
                if hmac.compare_digest(actual_proof, expected_proof):
                    result = {
                        "status": "ok",
                        "pid": data.get("pid"),
                        "version": data.get("version"),
                        "identity_verified": True,
                    }
                    with _VERIFIED_DAEMON_LOCK:
                        _VERIFIED_DAEMON_CACHE[key] = (time.monotonic() + 30.0, result)
                    return result
                else:
                    sys.stderr.write(
                        f"[WARNING] a process on port {port} failed Mesh identity verification.\n"
                    )
            else:
                sys.stderr.write(
                    f"[WARNING] a process on port {port} failed Mesh identity verification.\n"
                )
    except (http.client.HTTPException, TimeoutError, OSError, ValueError):
        pass
    finally:
        try:
            conn.close()
        except Exception:
            pass

    with _VERIFIED_DAEMON_LOCK:
        _VERIFIED_DAEMON_CACHE.pop(key, None)
    return None


def trigger_daemon_unload(
    host: str = DEFAULT_HOST,
    port: int = DEFAULT_PORT,
) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
    if not verify_daemon_identity(host=host, port=port, timeout=0.5):
        return None
    try:
        headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json", **get_auth_headers()}
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            f"http://{host}:{port}/unload",
            data=b"{}",
            headers=headers,
            method="POST",
        )
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=3.0) as resp:
            if resp.status == 200:
                return json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
    except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError, ValueError):
        invalidate_daemon_identity_cache(host, port)
    return None


def _read_pid_file() -> Optional[int]:
    pid_file = _pid_file()
    if not pid_file.exists():
        return None
    try:
        reject_symlink(pid_file)
        raw = pid_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
        return int(raw) if raw else None
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return None


def _write_pid_file(pid: int) -> None:
    try:
        _write_private_file(_pid_file(), str(pid))
    except OSError:
        pass


def _remove_pid_file() -> None:
    try:
        _pid_file().unlink(missing_ok=True)
    except OSError:
        pass


def _is_pid_alive(pid: int) -> bool:
    if pid <= 0:
        return False
    if os.name == "nt":
        try:
            out = subprocess.check_output(
                ["tasklist", "/FI", f"PID eq {pid}", "/NH"],
                stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0x08000000),
            ).decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
            return str(pid) in out
        except Exception:
            return False
    else:
        try:
            os.kill(pid, 0)
            return True
        except OSError:
            return False


def _matches_mesh_daemon_cmdline(cmdline: Union[str, list[str]]) -> bool:
    if isinstance(cmdline, (list, tuple)):
        norm = " ".join(str(c) for c in cmdline).strip()
    else:
        norm = (cmdline or "").strip()
    if not norm:
        return False

    normalized_slashes = norm.replace("\\", "/")
    try:
        tokens = [t.strip("\"'").lower() for t in shlex.split(normalized_slashes, posix=True) if t.strip()]
    except ValueError:
        tokens = [t.strip("\"'").lower() for t in normalized_slashes.split() if t.strip()]
    if not tokens:
        return False

    for idx, tok in enumerate(tokens[:-1]):
        if tok == "-m" and tokens[idx + 1] in ("mesh.http_server", "mesh.daemon"):
            return True
    if any(tok in ("mesh.http_server", "mesh.daemon") for tok in tokens):
        return True

    basenames = [t.rsplit("/", 1)[-1] for t in tokens]
    for base in basenames:
        stem = base[:-4] if base.endswith(".exe") else base
        if stem in ("mesh-http", "mesh-server-http", "mesh-daemon"):
            return True
    for tok in tokens:
        if tok.endswith("/mesh/http_server.py") or tok == "mesh/http_server.py":
            return True

    for idx, base in enumerate(basenames[:-1]):
        stem = base[:-4] if base.endswith(".exe") else base
        if stem == "mesh" and tokens[idx + 1] in ("http", "daemon"):
            return True

    return False


def _is_mesh_daemon_process(pid: int) -> bool:
    if not _is_pid_alive(pid):
        return False
    if os.name == "nt":
        try:
            out = subprocess.check_output(
                [
                    "powershell",
                    "-NoProfile",
                    "-Command",
                    f"(Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter 'ProcessId = {pid}').CommandLine",
                ],
                stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0x08000000),
                timeout=2.5,
            ).decode("utf-8", errors="ignore").strip()
            if out:
                return _matches_mesh_daemon_cmdline(out)
            return False
        except Exception:
            return False

    proc_dir = Path(f"/proc/{pid}")
    if proc_dir.exists():
        try:
            if hasattr(os, "getuid") and proc_dir.stat().st_uid != os.getuid():
                return False
            raw_bytes = (proc_dir / "cmdline").read_bytes()
            args = [a.decode("utf-8", errors="ignore") for a in raw_bytes.split(b"\x00") if a]
            cmdline_str = " ".join(shlex.quote(a) for a in args)
            return _matches_mesh_daemon_cmdline(cmdline_str)
        except OSError:
            return False

    try:
        if hasattr(os, "getuid"):
            uid_out = subprocess.check_output(
                ["ps", "-p", str(pid), "-o", "uid="],
                stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                timeout=2.0,
            ).decode("utf-8", errors="ignore").strip()
            if uid_out and int(uid_out) != os.getuid():
                return False

        out = subprocess.check_output(
            ["ps", "-p", str(pid), "-o", "command="],
            stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            timeout=2.0,
        ).decode("utf-8", errors="ignore").strip()
        return _matches_mesh_daemon_cmdline(out)
    except Exception:
        return False


def _resolve_background_python() -> str:
    exe = Path(sys.executable)
    if os.name == "nt" and exe.name.lower() == "python.exe":
        pythonw = exe.with_name("pythonw.exe")
        if pythonw.exists():
            return str(pythonw)
    return str(exe)


def ensure_daemon_running(
    host: str = DEFAULT_HOST,
    port: int = DEFAULT_PORT,
    idle_timeout: float = DEFAULT_IDLE_TIMEOUT,
    wait: bool = False,
    interactive_prompt: bool = True,
    assume_yes: bool = False,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    global _LAST_SPAWN_TS
    if os.environ.get("MESH_IN_DAEMON", "").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes"):
        return {
            "running": True,
            "already_running": True,
            "pid": os.getpid(),
            "host": host,
            "port": port,
        }
    if os.environ.get("MESH_NO_AUTO_DAEMON", "").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes"):
        return {"running": False, "declined": True, "host": host, "port": port}

    ident = verify_daemon_identity(host=host, port=port, timeout=0.25)
    if ident is not None:
        return {
            "running": True,
            "already_running": True,
            "pid": ident.get("pid"),
            "host": host,
            "port": port,
            "health": ident,
        }

    with _SPAWN_LOCK:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if not wait and (now - _LAST_SPAWN_TS) < 4.0:
            pid = _read_pid_file()
            return {"running": False, "spawning": True, "pid": pid, "host": host, "port": port}
        _LAST_SPAWN_TS = now

    if interactive_prompt and not request_user_permission(
        f"start the background Mesh HTTP daemon on {host}:{port}",
        assume_yes=assume_yes,
    ):
        return {"running": False, "declined": True, "host": host, "port": port}

    return start_daemon(
        host=host,
        port=port,
        idle_timeout=idle_timeout,
        lazy=False,
        wait=wait,
        interactive_prompt=False,
        assume_yes=True,
    )


def start_daemon(
    host: str = DEFAULT_HOST,
    port: int = DEFAULT_PORT,
    idle_timeout: float = DEFAULT_IDLE_TIMEOUT,
    lazy: bool = False,
    wait: bool = True,
    interactive_prompt: bool = False,
    assume_yes: bool = False,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    existing_ident = verify_daemon_identity(
        host=host,
        port=port,
        timeout=0.4 if wait else 0.2,
    )
    if existing_ident is not None:
        pid = existing_ident.get("pid") or _read_pid_file()
        if pid:
            _write_pid_file(int(pid))
        return {
            "running": True,
            "already_running": True,
            "pid": pid,
            "host": host,
            "port": port,
            "health": existing_ident,
        }

    existing_pid = _read_pid_file()
    if existing_pid is not None:
        if _is_pid_alive(existing_pid) and _is_mesh_daemon_process(existing_pid):
            if wait:
                for i in range(40):
                    if not _is_pid_alive(existing_pid):
                        break
                    time.sleep(0.04 if i < 10 else 0.12)
                    verified_existing = verify_daemon_identity(host=host, port=port, timeout=0.35)
                    if verified_existing is not None:
                        return {
                            "running": True,
                            "already_running": True,
                            "pid": existing_pid,
                            "host": host,
                            "port": port,
                            "health": verified_existing,
                        }
            if _is_pid_alive(existing_pid):
                return {
                    "running": False,
                    "already_running": False,
                    "spawning": True,
                    "retryable": True,
                    "pid": existing_pid,
                    "host": host,
                    "port": port,
                    "health": None,
                    "error": (
                        f"Mesh daemon (PID {existing_pid}) is still starting up; "
                        "identity verification timed out. Retry shortly."
                    ),
                }
        _remove_pid_file()

    if interactive_prompt and not request_user_permission(
        f"start the background Mesh HTTP daemon on {host}:{port}",
        assume_yes=assume_yes,
    ):
        return {"running": False, "declined": True, "pid": None, "host": host, "port": port}

    ensure_secure_mesh_dir()
    get_or_create_auth_token()
    python_exe = _resolve_background_python()
    cmd = [
        python_exe,
        "-m",
        "mesh.http_server",
        "--host",
        host,
        "--port",
        str(port),
        "--idle-timeout",
        str(idle_timeout),
    ]
    uds_path = os.environ.get("MESH_UNIX_SOCKET", "").strip()
    if uds_path and os.name != "nt":
        cmd.extend(["--unix-socket", uds_path])
    if lazy:
        cmd.append("--no-warmup")

    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["MESH_HTTP_HOST"] = host
    env["MESH_HTTP_PORT"] = str(port)
    env["MESH_IDLE_UNLOAD_SECONDS"] = str(idle_timeout)
    env["MESH_IN_DAEMON"] = "1"
    if assume_yes and not lazy:
        env["MESH_ALLOW_DAEMON_DOWNLOAD"] = "1"

    log_handle = _open_private_log_append(_log_file())
    try:
        if os.name == "nt":
            creationflags = (
                getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0x08000000)
                | getattr(subprocess, "DETACHED_PROCESS", 0x00000008)
                | getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP", 0x00000200)
            )
            proc = subprocess.Popen(
                cmd,
                stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                stdout=log_handle,
                stderr=log_handle,
                env=env,
                creationflags=creationflags,
                close_fds=True,
            )
        else:
            proc = subprocess.Popen(
                cmd,
                stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                stdout=log_handle,
                stderr=log_handle,
                env=env,
                start_new_session=True,
                close_fds=True,
            )
    finally:
        log_handle.close()

    if proc.poll() is None:
        _write_pid_file(proc.pid)

    if not wait:
        time.sleep(0.05)
        alive = proc.poll() is None
        if not alive and _read_pid_file() == proc.pid:
            _remove_pid_file()
        return {
            "running": alive,
            "already_running": False,
            "spawning": alive,
            "pid": proc.pid if alive else None,
            "host": host,
            "port": port,
            "health": None,
        }

    verified: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = None
    for i in range(40):
        if proc.poll() is not None:
            break
        time.sleep(0.04 if i < 10 else 0.12)
        verified = verify_daemon_identity(host=host, port=port, timeout=0.35)
        if verified is not None:
            break

    if verified is not None:
        _write_pid_file(proc.pid)
        return {
            "running": True,
            "already_running": False,
            "pid": proc.pid,
            "host": host,
            "port": port,
            "health": verified,
        }

    proc_dead = proc.poll() is not None
    if proc_dead:
        if _read_pid_file() == proc.pid:
            _remove_pid_file()
        return {
            "running": False,
            "already_running": False,
            "spawning": False,
            "retryable": False,
            "pid": None,
            "host": host,
            "port": port,
            "health": None,
            "error": "Daemon process exited before completing startup.",
        }

    return {
        "running": False,
        "already_running": False,
        "spawning": True,
        "retryable": True,
        "pid": proc.pid,
        "host": host,
        "port": port,
        "health": None,
        "error": (
            f"Daemon process (PID {proc.pid}) is still starting up after identity verification timeout; "
            "retry shortly."
        ),
    }


def stop_daemon(host: str = DEFAULT_HOST, port: int = DEFAULT_PORT) -> dict[str, Any]:
    pid_from_file = _read_pid_file()
    ident = verify_daemon_identity(host=host, port=port, timeout=0.5)
    pid_from_identity = (
        int(ident["pid"])
        if (ident and ident.get("identity_verified") and ident.get("pid"))
        else None
    )

    candidate_pid: Optional[int] = None
    if pid_from_file and _is_mesh_daemon_process(int(pid_from_file)):
        candidate_pid = int(pid_from_file)
    elif pid_from_identity and _is_mesh_daemon_process(int(pid_from_identity)):
        candidate_pid = int(pid_from_identity)
    else:
        raw_pid = pid_from_file or pid_from_identity
        _remove_pid_file()
        invalidate_daemon_identity_cache(host, port)
        if raw_pid:
            return {"stopped": False, "reason": "pid_not_mesh", "pid": int(raw_pid)}
        return {"stopped": False, "reason": "not_running", "pid": None}

    killed = False
    if os.name == "nt":
        try:
            subprocess.run(
                ["taskkill", "/PID", str(candidate_pid), "/T", "/F"],
                stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0x08000000),
                check=False,
            )
            killed = True
        except Exception:
            killed = False
    else:
        try:
            os.kill(candidate_pid, signal.SIGTERM)
            for _ in range(15):
                if not _is_pid_alive(candidate_pid):
                    break
                time.sleep(0.05)
            if _is_pid_alive(candidate_pid) and _is_mesh_daemon_process(candidate_pid):
                sigkill = getattr(signal, "SIGKILL", signal.SIGTERM)
                os.kill(candidate_pid, sigkill)
            killed = True
        except OSError:
            killed = False

    _remove_pid_file()
    invalidate_daemon_identity_cache(host, port)
    return {"stopped": killed, "pid": candidate_pid}


def install_startup(
    host: str = DEFAULT_HOST,
    port: int = DEFAULT_PORT,
    idle_timeout: float = DEFAULT_IDLE_TIMEOUT,
    start_now: bool = True,
    interactive_prompt: bool = False,
    assume_yes: bool = False,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if interactive_prompt and not request_user_permission(
        "register the Mesh daemon for OS login auto-start",
        assume_yes=assume_yes,
    ):
        return {"installed": False, "declined": True, "platform": sys.platform, "location": ""}

    python_exe = _resolve_background_python()
    cmd_args = [
        python_exe,
        "-m",
        "mesh.http_server",
        "--host",
        host,
        "--port",
        str(port),
        "--idle-timeout",
        str(idle_timeout),
        "--no-warmup",
    ]

    if os.name == "nt":
        import winreg

        quoted_cmd = subprocess.list2cmdline(cmd_args)
        with winreg.OpenKey(
            winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
            _WIN_RUN_KEY,
            0,
            winreg.KEY_SET_VALUE,
        ) as key:
            winreg.SetValueEx(key, _WIN_RUN_VALUE_NAME, 0, winreg.REG_SZ, quoted_cmd)
        loc = f"HKCU\\{_WIN_RUN_KEY}\\{_WIN_RUN_VALUE_NAME}"
        platform_name = "windows"
    elif sys.platform == "darwin":
        plist_dir = Path.home() / "Library" / "LaunchAgents"
        plist_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        plist_path = plist_dir / "com.mesh.daemon.plist"
        xml_attr_esc = {'"': "&quot;", "'": "&apos;"}
        args_xml = "\n".join(
            f"        <string>{xml_escape(str(a), xml_attr_esc)}</string>" for a in cmd_args
        )
        escaped_log = xml_escape(str(_log_file()), xml_attr_esc)
        plist_content = f"""<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE plist PUBLIC "-//Apple//DTD PLIST 1.0//EN" "http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd">
<plist version="1.0">
<dict>
    <key>Label</key>
    <string>com.mesh.daemon</string>
    <key>ProgramArguments</key>
    <array>
{args_xml}
    </array>
    <key>RunAtLoad</key>
    <true/>
    <key>KeepAlive</key>
    <false/>
    <key>StandardOutPath</key>
    <string>{escaped_log}</string>
    <key>StandardErrorPath</key>
    <string>{escaped_log}</string>
</dict>
</plist>
"""
        plist_path.write_text(plist_content, encoding="utf-8")
        loc = str(plist_path)
        platform_name = "macos"
    else:
        autostart_dir = Path.home() / ".config" / "autostart"
        autostart_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        desktop_path = autostart_dir / "mesh-daemon.desktop"
        exec_line = _format_desktop_exec_command([str(a) for a in cmd_args])
        desktop_content = (
            "[Desktop Entry]\n"
            "Type=Application\n"
            "Name=Mesh Context Daemon\n"
            f"Exec={exec_line}\n"
            "Hidden=false\n"
            "NoDisplay=true\n"
            "X-GNOME-Autostart-enabled=true\n"
        )
        desktop_path.write_text(desktop_content, encoding="utf-8")
        loc = str(desktop_path)
        platform_name = "linux"

    try:
        _write_private_file(_autostart_marker_file(), "1")
    except OSError:
        pass

    daemon_res = None
    if start_now:
        daemon_res = start_daemon(host=host, port=port, idle_timeout=idle_timeout, lazy=True)

    return {
        "installed": True,
        "platform": platform_name,
        "location": loc,
        "command": _format_desktop_exec_command([str(a) for a in cmd_args]),
        "daemon": daemon_res,
    }


def remove_startup() -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        _autostart_marker_file().unlink(missing_ok=True)
    except OSError:
        pass
    if os.name == "nt":
        import winreg

        removed = False
        try:
            with winreg.OpenKey(
                winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
                _WIN_RUN_KEY,
                0,
                winreg.KEY_SET_VALUE,
            ) as key:
                winreg.DeleteValue(key, _WIN_RUN_VALUE_NAME)
                removed = True
        except FileNotFoundError:
            removed = False
        return {"removed": removed, "platform": "windows"}

    if sys.platform == "darwin":
        plist_path = Path.home() / "Library" / "LaunchAgents" / "com.mesh.daemon.plist"
        existed = plist_path.exists()
        plist_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        return {"removed": existed, "platform": "macos"}

    desktop_path = Path.home() / ".config" / "autostart" / "mesh-daemon.desktop"
    existed = desktop_path.exists()
    desktop_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    return {"removed": existed, "platform": "linux"}


def is_startup_installed() -> bool:
    if os.name == "nt":
        try:
            import winreg

            with winreg.OpenKey(
                winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
                _WIN_RUN_KEY,
                0,
                winreg.KEY_READ,
            ) as key:
                val, _ = winreg.QueryValueEx(key, _WIN_RUN_VALUE_NAME)
                return bool(val)
        except OSError:
            return False
    if sys.platform == "darwin":
        return (Path.home() / "Library" / "LaunchAgents" / "com.mesh.daemon.plist").exists()
    return (Path.home() / ".config" / "autostart" / "mesh-daemon.desktop").exists()


def daemon_cmd() -> None:
    """Entry point for: mesh-daemon / mesh daemon[cite: 2]"""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Manage background Mesh daemon and auto-start.")
    parser.add_argument(
        "action",
        nargs="?",
        default="status",
        choices=[
            "start",
            "stop",
            "restart",
            "status",
            "unload",
            "warmup",
            "rotate-token",
            "install-startup",
            "remove-startup",
        ],
    )
    parser.add_argument("--host", default=DEFAULT_HOST)
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=DEFAULT_PORT)
    parser.add_argument("--idle-timeout", type=float, default=DEFAULT_IDLE_TIMEOUT)
    parser.add_argument("--lazy", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--yes", "-y", action="store_true", help="Skip interactive y/n permission prompts")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    from . import ui

    if args.action == "rotate-token":
        rotate_auth_token()
        ui.success("Rotated local Mesh authentication token", str(_auth_token_file()))
        return

    if args.action == "start":
        if not request_user_permission(
            f"start the background Mesh HTTP daemon on {args.host}:{args.port}",
            assume_yes=args.yes,
        ):
            ui.step("Cancelled — daemon was not started")
            return
        from .memory import _has_local_onnx_pair, ensure_model_downloaded

        if _has_local_onnx_pair() is None and request_user_permission(
            "download the ONNX embedding model in the background",
            assume_yes=args.yes,
        ):
            ensure_model_downloaded(blocking=False, assume_yes=True, detached=True)
        res = start_daemon(
            host=args.host,
            port=args.port,
            idle_timeout=args.idle_timeout,
            lazy=args.lazy,
        )
        if not res.get("running"):
            ui.error(
                "Failed to start Mesh daemon",
                f"Check the log: {_log_file()}",
            )
            sys.exit(1)
        if res.get("already_running"):
            ui.info(
                "Mesh daemon already running",
                f"http://{args.host}:{args.port}  {ui.sym('dot')}  pid {res['pid']}",
            )
        else:
            ui.success(
                "Started Mesh daemon",
                f"http://{args.host}:{args.port}  {ui.sym('dot')}  pid {res['pid']}",
            )

    elif args.action == "warmup":
        if not request_user_permission(
            f"download the ONNX model and start the background Mesh daemon on {args.host}:{args.port}",
            assume_yes=args.yes,
        ):
            ui.step("Cancelled — nothing was changed")
            return
        from .memory import ensure_model_downloaded, warmup_model

        ui.info("Ensuring the local ONNX embedding model is downloaded…")
        pair = ensure_model_downloaded(blocking=True, timeout=60.0, assume_yes=True)
        if pair:
            ui.success("ONNX model cached", str(pair[0].parent))
        warmup_model(assume_yes=True)
        res = start_daemon(
            host=args.host,
            port=args.port,
            idle_timeout=args.idle_timeout,
            lazy=False,
        )
        if not res.get("running"):
            ui.error(
                "Failed to start Mesh daemon",
                f"Check the log: {_log_file()}",
            )
            sys.exit(1)
        ui.success(
            "Mesh daemon warm and running",
            f"http://{args.host}:{args.port}  {ui.sym('dot')}  pid {res['pid']}",
        )

    elif args.action == "stop":
        res = stop_daemon(host=args.host, port=args.port)
        if res["stopped"]:
            ui.success("Stopped Mesh daemon", f"pid {res['pid']}")
        elif res.get("reason") == "pid_not_mesh":
            ui.info(
                "Removed stale PID file",
                f"PID {res['pid']} is not a Mesh daemon",
            )
        else:
            ui.info("Mesh daemon is not running")

    elif args.action == "restart":
        if not request_user_permission(
            f"restart the background Mesh HTTP daemon on {args.host}:{args.port}",
            assume_yes=args.yes,
        ):
            ui.step("Cancelled — daemon was not restarted")
            return
        stop_daemon(host=args.host, port=args.port)
        res = start_daemon(
            host=args.host,
            port=args.port,
            idle_timeout=args.idle_timeout,
            lazy=args.lazy,
        )
        if not res.get("running"):
            ui.error(
                "Failed to restart Mesh daemon",
                f"Check the log: {_log_file()}",
            )
            sys.exit(1)
        ui.success(
            "Restarted Mesh daemon",
            f"http://{args.host}:{args.port}  {ui.sym('dot')}  pid {res['pid']}",
        )

    elif args.action == "unload":
        unload_res = trigger_daemon_unload(host=args.host, port=args.port)
        if unload_res is None:
            ui.info("Mesh daemon is not running")
        else:
            ui.success("Unloaded neural model weights from RAM")

    elif args.action == "install-startup":
        info = install_startup(
            host=args.host,
            port=args.port,
            idle_timeout=args.idle_timeout,
            interactive_prompt=True,
            assume_yes=args.yes,
        )
        if info.get("declined"):
            ui.step("Cancelled — login auto-start was not registered")
            return
        daemon_res = info.get("daemon") or {}
        if daemon_res.get("running"):
            ui.success(
                "Registered Mesh daemon for OS login and started it",
                str(info["platform"]),
            )
        else:
            ui.success("Registered Mesh daemon for OS login", str(info["platform"]))
        ui.kv([("Location", info["location"])])

    elif args.action == "remove-startup":
        info = remove_startup()
        if info["removed"]:
            ui.success("Removed login auto-start registration", str(info["platform"]))
        else:
            ui.info("Login auto-start was not registered")

    elif args.action == "status":
        ident = verify_daemon_identity(host=args.host, port=args.port)
        auto_start = "enabled" if is_startup_installed() else "disabled"
        if not ident:
            health = get_daemon_health(host=args.host, port=args.port)
            ui.header("Daemon", f"{args.host}:{args.port}")
            if health:
                ui.warn(
                    "Unverified process is listening on this port",
                    "The HMAC token challenge failed. Stop it before trusting this endpoint.",
                )
            else:
                ui.step("Not running")
            ui.kv([("Login auto-start", auto_start)])
            print()
            return
        eng: dict[str, Any] = {}
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(
                f"http://{args.host}:{args.port}/engine",
                headers=get_auth_headers(),
                method="GET",
            )
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=1.0) as resp:
                if resp.status == 200:
                    data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
                    eng = data.get("engine") or {}
        except Exception:
            invalidate_daemon_identity_cache(args.host, args.port)
        loaded_str = "loaded (warm)" if eng.get("model_loaded") else "sleeping (~0 MB model RAM)"
        ui.header("Daemon", f"{args.host}:{args.port}", "running")
        ui.kv(
            [
                ("PID", ident.get("pid")),
                ("Identity", "verified (HMAC-SHA256)"),
                ("Model state", loaded_str),
                ("Engine backend", eng.get("backend", "unloaded")),
                ("ONNX cached", eng.get("onnx_cached_on_disk", False)),
                ("Login auto-start", auto_start),
            ]
        )
        print()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    daemon_cmd()

=== FILE: mesh/digest.py (LINES: 457) ===
"""
mesh/digest.py — Timezone-safe End-of-Session Digest for Mesh.

Summarizes recent activity: what was learned, contradictions detected, and stale memories.
Security & Consistency:
  - Validates namespace identifiers via `validate_namespace()` and rejects symlinks (`reject_symlink`)
  - Flushes buffered telemetry (`flush_all_telemetry`) before reading database state
  - Decrypts encrypted-at-rest fields transparently via `mesh.crypto` (Finding S5)
  - Excludes `quarantined=1` memories from active digest counts by default (Finding S8)
  - Reads unresolved contradictions from the `contradictions` table (falling back to `audit_log`
    only when the `contradictions` table is absent) so resolved conflicts are not re-reported (Finding S12)
  - Redacts `local_only=True` memory content by default unless `include_private=True`
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import sqlite3
import sys
from datetime import timedelta
from typing import Any

from .audit import (
    REDACTED_CONTENT_PLACEHOLDER,
    _connect_audit,
    _get_audit_db_path,
    ensure_secure_mesh_dir,
    reject_symlink,
    validate_namespace,
)
from .crypto import decrypt_text_if_encrypted
from .store import (
    MeshStore,
    _compute_row_effective_confidence,
    _parse_iso_utc,
    _utc_now,
    flush_all_telemetry,
)


def _has_table(conn: sqlite3.Connection, table_name: str) -> bool:
    try:
        row = conn.execute(
            "SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name = ?",
            (table_name,),
        ).fetchone()
        return row is not None
    except sqlite3.Error:
        return False


def generate_digest(
    namespace: str = "shared",
    hours: int = 24,
    include_private: bool = False,
    filter_stale_by_window: bool = False,
    include_quarantined: bool = False,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Generate a session digest for the given namespace and time window using timezone-safe UTC timestamps.
    Never creates a `.db` file if the namespace does not exist.
    """
    clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    flush_all_telemetry()
    safe_hours = max(1, int(hours or 24))
    now = _utc_now()
    since_dt = now - timedelta(hours=safe_hours)
    since_iso = since_dt.isoformat()

    base_dir = ensure_secure_mesh_dir().resolve()
    raw_candidate = base_dir / f"mesh_{clean_ns}.db"
    reject_symlink(raw_candidate)
    db_path = raw_candidate.resolve()
    if db_path.parent != base_dir:
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid namespace path resolution for '{namespace}'.")

    new_memories: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    stale_memories: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    contradictions: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    has_contradictions_table = False
    unparseable_count = 0
    quarantined_count = 0
    namespace_exists = db_path.exists()
    read_errors: list[str] = []

    if namespace_exists:
        store = None
        all_rows = []
        try:
            store = MeshStore(namespace=clean_ns, create=False)
            cache = store._get_vector_cache()
            if cache is not None and cache.rows_by_id:
                all_rows = sorted(
                    [dict(r) for r in cache.rows_by_id.values()],
                    key=lambda r: str(r.get("created_at") or ""),
                    reverse=True,
                )
        except Exception:
            store = None
            all_rows = []

        try:
            if store is not None and not all_rows:
                with store._connect() as conn:
                    if _has_table(conn, "memories"):
                        cols = {r[1] for r in conn.execute("PRAGMA table_info(memories)").fetchall()}
                        select_cols = (
                            "id, content, memory_type, source_agent, confidence, "
                            "created_at, decay_start_at, ttl_days, local_only"
                        )
                        if "quarantined" in cols:
                            select_cols += ", quarantined"
                        if "trust_tier" in cols:
                            select_cols += ", trust_tier"
                        cursor = conn.cursor()
                        cursor.execute(
                            f"""
                            SELECT {select_cols}
                            FROM memories
                            WHERE namespace = ?
                            ORDER BY created_at DESC
                            """,
                            (clean_ns,),
                        )
                        all_rows = [dict(r) for r in cursor.fetchall()]
                        cursor.close()

            for row in all_rows:
                is_quarantined = bool(row.get("quarantined", 0))
                if is_quarantined:
                    quarantined_count += 1
                    if not include_quarantined:
                        continue

                mid = str(row.get("id") or "")
                short_id = mid[:8]
                is_priv = bool(row.get("local_only"))
                plain_content = decrypt_text_if_encrypted(row.get("content", ""))
                display_content = (
                    REDACTED_CONTENT_PLACEHOLDER
                    if (is_priv and not include_private)
                    else plain_content
                )

                created_dt = _parse_iso_utc(row.get("created_at"))
                if created_dt is None:
                    unparseable_count += 1
                elif created_dt >= since_dt:
                    new_memories.append(
                        {
                            "id": mid,
                            "short_id": short_id,
                            "content": display_content,
                            "memory_type": row.get("memory_type", "fact"),
                            "source_agent": row.get("source_agent", "unknown"),
                            "confidence": float(row.get("confidence", 1.0)),
                            "created_at": row.get("created_at", ""),
                            "local_only": is_priv,
                            "trust_tier": row.get("trust_tier", "user"),
                            "quarantined": is_quarantined,
                        }
                    )

                eff_conf, is_stale = _compute_row_effective_confidence(row, now=now)
                if is_stale:
                    if filter_stale_by_window and (created_dt is None or created_dt < since_dt):
                        continue
                    stale_memories.append(
                        {
                            "id": mid,
                            "short_id": short_id,
                            "content": display_content,
                            "memory_type": row.get("memory_type", "fact"),
                            "created_at": row.get("created_at", ""),
                            "decay_start_at": row.get("decay_start_at"),
                            "ttl_days": row.get("ttl_days"),
                            "confidence": float(row.get("confidence", 1.0)),
                            "effective_confidence": eff_conf,
                            "local_only": is_priv,
                        }
                    )

            if store is not None:
                with store._connect() as conn:
                    if _has_table(conn, "contradictions"):
                        has_contradictions_table = True
                        c_cur = conn.execute(
                            """
                            SELECT id, new_content, existing_content, detected_at
                            FROM contradictions
                            WHERE namespace = ? AND COALESCE(resolved, 0) = 0
                            ORDER BY detected_at DESC
                            """,
                            (clean_ns,),
                        )
                        for cr in c_cur.fetchall():
                            det_dt = _parse_iso_utc(cr["detected_at"])
                            if det_dt is not None and det_dt >= since_dt:
                                new_c = decrypt_text_if_encrypted(cr["new_content"])
                                if not include_private and new_c == REDACTED_CONTENT_PLACEHOLDER:
                                    display_c = REDACTED_CONTENT_PLACEHOLDER
                                else:
                                    display_c = new_c
                                contradictions.append(
                                    {
                                        "id": cr["id"],
                                        "content": display_c,
                                        "agent_id": "memory-store",
                                        "timestamp": cr["detected_at"],
                                    }
                                )
                        c_cur.close()
        except sqlite3.Error as exc:
            read_errors.append(f"memory database: {exc}")

    audit_db = _get_audit_db_path(clean_ns)
    recalls = 0
    agents_active: list[str] = []

    if audit_db.exists():
        try:
            conn = _connect_audit(audit_db)
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            if _has_table(conn, "audit_log"):
                cursor = conn.cursor()
                sql_floor = (since_dt - timedelta(days=1)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
                cursor.execute(
                    """
                    SELECT action, content, agent_id, timestamp, had_conflict
                    FROM audit_log
                    WHERE namespace = ? AND timestamp >= ?
                    ORDER BY timestamp DESC
                    """,
                    (clean_ns, sql_floor),
                )
                active_set: set[str] = set()
                for r in cursor.fetchall():
                    ts = _parse_iso_utc(r["timestamp"])
                    if ts is None or ts < since_dt:
                        continue
                    act = r["action"]
                    aid = r["agent_id"]
                    if aid:
                        active_set.add(str(aid))
                    if act == "recall":
                        recalls += 1
                    elif (
                        not has_contradictions_table
                        and act == "learn"
                        and int(r["had_conflict"] or 0) == 1
                    ):
                        contradictions.append(
                            {
                                "content": decrypt_text_if_encrypted(r["content"]),
                                "agent_id": aid,
                                "timestamp": r["timestamp"],
                            }
                        )
                cursor.close()
                agents_active = sorted(active_set)
        except sqlite3.Error as exc:
            read_errors.append(f"audit log: {exc}")

    return {
        "namespace": clean_ns,
        "hours": safe_hours,
        "since": since_iso,
        "new_memories": new_memories,
        "new_count": len(new_memories),
        "quarantined_count": quarantined_count,
        "unparseable_count": unparseable_count,
        "contradictions": contradictions,
        "contradiction_count": len(contradictions),
        "stale_memories": stale_memories,
        "stale_count": len(stale_memories),
        "total_recalls": recalls,
        "agents_active": agents_active,
        "namespace_exists": namespace_exists,
        "read_errors": read_errors,
    }


def _one_line(text: Any, max_len: int = 75) -> str:
    clean = " ".join(str(text or "").split())
    return clean[:max_len]


def print_digest(digest: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Print a human-readable digest to stdout."""
    from . import ui

    hours = int(digest["hours"])
    window = f"last {hours}h" if hours != 24 else "last 24h"
    ns = digest["namespace"]
    width = ui.term_width()

    ui.header("Digest", ns, window)

    if not digest.get("namespace_exists", True) and not (
        digest["new_count"]
        or digest["contradiction_count"]
        or digest["stale_count"]
        or digest["total_recalls"]
    ):
        ui.hint(f"Namespace '{ns}' has no memories yet.", stream=sys.stdout)
        print()
        return

    # ── What's new ──
    ui.section(f"New  {ui.sym('dot')}  {digest['new_count']}")
    if digest["new_count"] == 0:
        ui.hint(f"Nothing stored in the {window}.", stream=sys.stdout)
    else:
        print()
        rows = []
        for mem in digest["new_memories"][:10]:
            flag = ("private", "accent") if mem.get("local_only") else ""
            rows.append(
                [
                    mem.get("short_id") or str(mem.get("id") or "")[:8],
                    (str(mem.get("memory_type", "fact")), "info"),
                    (ui.relative_time(mem.get("created_at")), "muted"),
                    (str(mem.get("source_agent", "unknown")), "muted"),
                    flag,
                    " ".join(str(mem.get("content") or "").split()),
                ]
            )
        ui.table(["ID", "Type", "When", "By", "", "Memory"], rows, flex=5)
        if digest["new_count"] > 10:
            ui.hint(f"…and {digest['new_count'] - 10} more. Browse them with `mesh inspect`.", stream=sys.stdout)

    if int(digest.get("quarantined_count") or 0) > 0:
        q_cnt = int(digest["quarantined_count"])
        ui.warn(
            f"{ui.plural(q_cnt, 'quarantined memory', 'quarantined memories')} awaiting review",
            "Run `mesh inspect --quarantined` and `mesh approve <id>`.",
            stdout=True,
        )

    unparseable = int(digest.get("unparseable_count") or 0)
    if unparseable > 0:
        ui.warn(
            f"{ui.plural(unparseable, 'memory', 'memories')} skipped because the timestamp couldn't be read",
            stdout=True,
        )

    # ── Activity ──
    if digest["total_recalls"] > 0:
        agents = ", ".join(digest["agents_active"]) if digest["agents_active"] else "unknown"
        ui.section("Activity")
        ui.kv(
            [
                ("Searches", f"{digest['total_recalls']}"),
                ("Active agents", agents),
            ],
            indent=ui.IND + "  ",
        )

    # ── Contradictions ──
    if digest["contradiction_count"] > 0:
        ui.section(f"Contradictions  {ui.sym('dot')}  {digest['contradiction_count']}")
        print()
        for c in digest["contradictions"]:
            ts = ui.relative_time(c.get("timestamp"))
            lines = ui.wrap(_one_line(c.get("content"), 300), width - 6, max_lines=2)
            print(f"{ui.IND}  {ui.style(ui.sym('warn'), 'warn', 'bold')} {lines[0]}")
            for extra in lines[1:]:
                print(f"{ui.IND}    {extra}")
            print(f"{ui.IND}    {ui.style('flagged by ' + str(c.get('agent_id') or 'unknown') + (' ' + ui.sym('dot') + ' ' + ts if ts else ''), 'muted')}")
        ui.hint("Resolve them with `mesh conflicts`", stream=sys.stdout)

    # ── Stale ──
    if digest["stale_count"] > 0:
        ui.section(f"Going stale  {ui.sym('dot')}  {digest['stale_count']}")
        print()
        rows = []
        for mem in digest["stale_memories"][:5]:
            why = f"TTL {mem['ttl_days']}d expired" if mem.get("ttl_days") is not None else "low confidence"
            rows.append(
                [
                    mem.get("short_id") or str(mem.get("id") or "")[:8],
                    (why, "warn"),
                    (ui.relative_time(mem.get("created_at")), "muted"),
                    " ".join(str(mem.get("content") or "").split()),
                ]
            )
        ui.table(["ID", "Why", "Stored", "Memory"], rows, flex=3)
        if digest["stale_count"] > 5:
            ui.hint(f"…and {digest['stale_count'] - 5} more.", stream=sys.stdout)
        ui.next_steps(
            [
                ("mesh update <id>", "refresh a memory that's still true"),
                ("mesh forget <id>", "remove one that isn't"),
            ]
        )

    read_errors = digest.get("read_errors") or []
    if read_errors:
        ui.warn(
            "Some Mesh data could not be read; this digest may be incomplete",
            "\n".join(str(err) for err in read_errors),
            stdout=True,
        )

    if (
        digest.get("namespace_exists", True)
        and not read_errors
        and digest["new_count"] == 0
        and digest["contradiction_count"] == 0
        and digest["stale_count"] == 0
        and int(digest.get("unparseable_count") or 0) == 0
        and int(digest.get("quarantined_count") or 0) == 0
    ):
        print()
        ui.success("All clear — Mesh is up to date")
    print()


def digest_cmd() -> None:
    """Entry point for: mesh-digest / mesh digest"""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog="mesh digest",
        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
        description="Show a digest of recent Mesh activity.",
        epilog="""Examples:
  mesh digest                  # Last 24 hours
  mesh digest --hours 2        # Last 2 hours
  mesh digest --namespace work
  mesh digest --json           # Machine-readable output
        """,
    )
    parser.add_argument("--namespace", default=None)
    parser.add_argument("--hours", type=int, default=24, help="Time window in hours (default: 24)")
    parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Output as JSON instead of human-readable")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--include-private",
        action="store_true",
        help="Include unredacted local_only memory content in the digest",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--window-stale",
        action="store_true",
        help="Filter stale memories to only those created within the time window",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()
    ns = validate_namespace(args.namespace or os.environ.get("MESH_NAMESPACE", "shared"))
    digest = generate_digest(
        namespace=ns,
        hours=args.hours,
        include_private=args.include_private,
        filter_stale_by_window=args.window_stale,
    )
    if args.json:
        print(json.dumps(digest, indent=2))
    else:
        print_digest(digest)
