=== FILE: mesh/guards.py (LINES: 335) ===
"""
mesh/guards.py — Ingestion guards for the Mesh context layer.

Sanitizes input, detects adversarial prompt injection patterns (across NFKC,
homoglyphs, inline markdown, paraphrases, and multilingual overrides),
validates tags, and auto-redacts credentials locally while enforcing `local_only=True`.

Addresses Findings:
  - S2, S3, S4: Uses unified `mesh.secrets` detection and local redaction
  - S8: Confusable homoglyph folding, inline markdown stripping, and expanded poison detection
  - S10: Zero private imports from `passive.py`; uses `redact_secrets_local` (preserving emails)
  - S11: Idempotent on already-redacted placeholders (`--force-public` safe) + `guard_tags()`
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections import OrderedDict
import re
import threading
import unicodedata
from typing import Iterable, NamedTuple, Optional

from .c_bridge import c_guard_prescan as _c_guard_prescan

from .secrets import (
    INVISIBLE_CHAR_CLASS,
    ZERO_WIDTH_RE,
    is_credential,
    redact_secrets_local,
)

_GUARD_INGEST_CACHE: OrderedDict[tuple[str, bool], "GuardResult"] = OrderedDict()
_POISON_CACHE: OrderedDict[str, bool] = OrderedDict()
_TAGS_CACHE: OrderedDict[tuple[tuple[str, ...], bool], tuple[list[str], bool, tuple[str, ...]]] = OrderedDict()
_GUARD_CACHE_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_MAX_GUARD_CACHE = 2048


def clear_guard_caches() -> None:
    with _GUARD_CACHE_LOCK:
        _GUARD_INGEST_CACHE.clear()
        _POISON_CACHE.clear()
        _TAGS_CACHE.clear()

_INVISIBLE_CHAR_CLASS = INVISIBLE_CHAR_CLASS
_ZERO_WIDTH_RE = ZERO_WIDTH_RE

# Strip control characters (C0/C1) except newline (\n) and tab (\t), plus invisible formatting chars
_CONTROL_CHAR_RE = re.compile(
    "[\x00-\x08\x0b\x0c\x0e-\x1f\x7f-\x9f" + INVISIBLE_CHAR_CLASS + "]"
)

# Finding S8: Confusable Cyrillic / Greek homoglyphs mapped to ASCII Latin equivalents
_CONFUSABLE_MAP = str.maketrans(
    {
        "\u0430": "a", "\u0410": "A",  # Cyrillic a
        "\u0435": "e", "\u0415": "E",  # Cyrillic e
        "\u0456": "i", "\u0406": "I",  # Cyrillic i
        "\u043e": "o", "\u041e": "O",  # Cyrillic o
        "\u0440": "p", "\u0420": "P",  # Cyrillic er -> p
        "\u0441": "c", "\u0421": "C",  # Cyrillic es -> c
        "\u0443": "y", "\u0423": "Y",  # Cyrillic u -> y
        "\u0445": "x", "\u0425": "X",  # Cyrillic ha -> x
        "\u0455": "s", "\u0405": "S",  # Cyrillic dze -> s
        "\u0458": "j", "\u0408": "J",  # Cyrillic je -> j
        "\u03bf": "o", "\u039f": "O",  # Greek omicron -> o
        "\u03b1": "a", "\u0391": "A",  # Greek alpha -> a
        "\u03b5": "e", "\u0395": "E",  # Greek epsilon -> e
        "\u03b9": "i", "\u0399": "I",  # Greek iota -> i
        "\u03ba": "k", "\u039a": "K",  # Greek kappa -> k
        "\u03c1": "p", "\u03a1": "P",  # Greek rho -> p
        "\u03c4": "t", "\u03a4": "T",  # Greek tau -> t
    }
)

# Finding S8: Strip inline markdown formatting (`**`, `*`, `__`, `_`, `` ` ``, `~~`) used to split words
_INLINE_MARKUP_BETWEEN_LETTERS_RE = re.compile(r"(?<=[A-Za-z])(?:[*_`~]{1,3})(?=[A-Za-z])")

# Finding S8: Expanded prompt-injection / instruction-override / structural breakout patterns
_POISON_PATTERNS = [
    # English override / ignore / disregard / forget / bypass with up to 4 qualifiers
    r"(?i)\b(?:ignore|disregard|forget|override|bypass)\s+"
    r"(?:(?:all|any|the|every|previous|prior|above|earlier|preceding|system|developer|user|safety|security|initial|original|existing)\s+){1,5}"
    r"(?:instructions?|prompts?|rules?|directives?|guidelines?|constraints?|guardrails?|directions?|commands?|policies)\b",
    r"(?i)\bdisregard\s+(?:all\s+|any\s+|the\s+)?(?:previous|prior|above|system|earlier|preceding)\b",
    # Paraphrase overrides ("do not follow earlier directives", "important system override")
    r"(?i)\b(?:do\s+not|don't|never)\s+(?:follow|obey|adhere\s+to)\s+"
    r"(?:(?:any|all|the|previous|prior|above|earlier|preceding|system|developer)\s+){1,4}"
    r"(?:instructions?|prompts?|rules?|directives?|guidelines?|constraints?)\b",
    r"(?i)\b(?:important|critical|urgent)\s+(?:system\s+|security\s+)?override\b",
    r"(?i)\byou\s+are\s+now\s+(?:in\s+)?(?:developer|dan|unrestricted|jailbreak|god)\s+mode\b",
    # Structural prompt / ChatML / XML breakout tags (Finding S8 / N7)
    r"(?i)</?\s*mesh_?context\b",
    r"(?i)</?\s*system(?:_?prompt|_?instructions)?\s*>",
    r"(?i)<\|(?:im_start|im_end|system|user|assistant|endoftext)\|>",
    r"(?i)\[/?INST\]",
    r"(?i)\bsystem\s*_?prompt\s*:",
    # Multilingual prompt injection overrides (Spanish, French, German, Portuguese)
    r"(?i)\b(?:ignora|ignorez|ignoriere|ignorem)\s+"
    r"(?:todas?\s+|toutes?\s+|alle\s+)?(?:las?\s+|les?\s+|as\s+)?"
    r"(?:instrucciones|instructions|anweisungen|instruções|reglas|règles|regeln)\s*"
    r"(?:anteriores|précédentes|vorherigen|anteriores)?\b",
]

_COMPILED_POISON = [re.compile(p) for p in _POISON_PATTERNS]


def _normalize_for_poison_check(text: str) -> str:
    """
    Normalize text for adversarial prompt-injection detection (Finding S8):
      1. Remove invisible format / zero-width characters.
      2. Apply Unicode NFKC normalization (converts fullwidth Latin to ASCII).
      3. Strip inline markdown emphasis/backticks splitting letters (`Ig**no**re` -> `Ignore`).
      4. Fold Cyrillic/Greek confusable homoglyphs to Latin (`Ign\u043ere` -> `Ignore`).
    """
    s = ZERO_WIDTH_RE.sub("", str(text or ""))
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", s)
    # Apply twice in case of nested inline markers
    s = _INLINE_MARKUP_BETWEEN_LETTERS_RE.sub("", s)
    s = _INLINE_MARKUP_BETWEEN_LETTERS_RE.sub("", s)
    return s.translate(_CONFUSABLE_MAP)


def is_poison_attempt(text: str) -> bool:
    """Return True if `text` matches any prompt-injection or structural breakout pattern."""
    # Fast-path: C pre-scanner guarantees no trigger tokens and clean ASCII — skip regex.
    if isinstance(text, str) and 0 < len(text.strip()) <= 16384 and _c_guard_prescan(text):
        return False

    if isinstance(text, str) and len(text) <= 4096:
        with _GUARD_CACHE_LOCK:
            res = _POISON_CACHE.get(text)
            if res is not None:
                _POISON_CACHE.move_to_end(text)
                return res

    norm = _normalize_for_poison_check(text)
    if not norm:
        res = False
    else:
        res = any(p.search(norm) for p in _COMPILED_POISON)

    if isinstance(text, str) and len(text) <= 4096:
        with _GUARD_CACHE_LOCK:
            if len(_POISON_CACHE) >= _MAX_GUARD_CACHE:
                _POISON_CACHE.popitem(last=False)
            _POISON_CACHE[text] = res
    return res


_is_poison_attempt = is_poison_attempt


class GuardResult(NamedTuple):
    content: str
    local_only: bool
    rejected: bool
    reason: Optional[str] = None
    warnings: tuple[str, ...] = ()


def _max_content_len() -> int:
    from .memory import MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH

    return int(MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH)


def guard_ingest(content: str, local_only: bool = False) -> GuardResult:
    """
    Validate and sanitize content before memory ingestion across all entry points:
      1. Strip NUL, unexpected C0/C1 control characters, and invisible characters (preserving \\n and \\t).
      2. Enforce `MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH` before running regexes (DoS defense).
      3. Reject adversarial prompt-injection / breakout patterns (`is_poison_attempt`).
      4. Detect credentials (`is_credential`) and redact secret values via `redact_secrets_local`
         (preserving non-secret emails locally) while forcing `local_only=True`.
    """
    if not isinstance(content, str):
        content = str(content or "")

    # Fast-path: C pre-scanner guarantees clean ASCII, no secret patterns, no injection tokens.
    # Skip all regex work and return accepted result immediately (< 0.002 ms).
    _stripped = content.strip()
    if _stripped and 0 < len(_stripped) <= min(16384, _max_content_len()) and _c_guard_prescan(content):
        return GuardResult(
            content=_stripped,
            local_only=bool(local_only),
            rejected=False,
            reason=None,
            warnings=(),
        )

    cache_key = (content, bool(local_only))
    if len(content) <= 4096:
        with _GUARD_CACHE_LOCK:
            cached = _GUARD_INGEST_CACHE.get(cache_key)
            if cached is not None:
                _GUARD_INGEST_CACHE.move_to_end(cache_key)
                return cached

    sanitized = _CONTROL_CHAR_RE.sub("", content).strip()
    if not sanitized:
        res = GuardResult(
            content="",
            local_only=bool(local_only),
            rejected=True,
            reason="Memory content cannot be empty or solely control characters.",
            warnings=(),
        )
        if len(content) <= 4096:
            with _GUARD_CACHE_LOCK:
                if len(_GUARD_INGEST_CACHE) >= _MAX_GUARD_CACHE:
                    _GUARD_INGEST_CACHE.popitem(last=False)
                _GUARD_INGEST_CACHE[cache_key] = res
        return res

    from .memory import MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH

    if len(sanitized) > MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH:
        res = GuardResult(
            content=sanitized,
            local_only=bool(local_only),
            rejected=True,
            reason=(
                f"content length ({len(sanitized)} chars) exceeds maximum allowed "
                f"length ({MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH} chars)."
            ),
            warnings=(),
        )
        if len(content) <= 4096:
            with _GUARD_CACHE_LOCK:
                if len(_GUARD_INGEST_CACHE) >= _MAX_GUARD_CACHE:
                    _GUARD_INGEST_CACHE.popitem(last=False)
                _GUARD_INGEST_CACHE[cache_key] = res
        return res

    if is_poison_attempt(sanitized):
        res = GuardResult(
            content=sanitized,
            local_only=bool(local_only),
            rejected=True,
            reason="Prompt injection or adversarial instruction pattern detected in memory content.",
            warnings=(),
        )
        if len(content) <= 4096:
            with _GUARD_CACHE_LOCK:
                if len(_GUARD_INGEST_CACHE) >= _MAX_GUARD_CACHE:
                    _GUARD_INGEST_CACHE.popitem(last=False)
                _GUARD_INGEST_CACHE[cache_key] = res
        return res

    warnings: list[str] = []
    final_local_only = bool(local_only)
    final_content = sanitized

    # Finding S2, S3, S4, S10, S11: Only flag and redact live credentials (not already-redacted placeholders)
    if is_credential(sanitized):
        final_content = redact_secrets_local(sanitized)
        final_local_only = True
        warnings.append(
            "Credential or secret pattern detected: secret was redacted and forced to private (local_only)."
        )

    res = GuardResult(
        content=final_content,
        local_only=final_local_only,
        rejected=False,
        reason=None,
        warnings=tuple(warnings),
    )
    if len(content) <= 4096:
        with _GUARD_CACHE_LOCK:
            if len(_GUARD_INGEST_CACHE) >= _MAX_GUARD_CACHE:
                _GUARD_INGEST_CACHE.popitem(last=False)
            _GUARD_INGEST_CACHE[cache_key] = res
    return res


def guard_tags(
    tags: Optional[Iterable[str]],
    local_only: bool = False,
) -> tuple[list[str], bool, tuple[str, ...]]:
    """
    Validate and sanitize memory tags against prompt injection and embedded secrets (Finding S11).
    Raises `ValueError` if any tag contains a prompt-injection payload or exceeds 128 chars.
    Redacts any secret inside a tag and sets `local_only=True`.
    """
    if not tags:
        return [], bool(local_only), ()

    tags_tuple: Optional[tuple[str, ...]] = None
    cache_key: Optional[tuple[tuple[str, ...], bool]] = None
    if isinstance(tags, (list, tuple)):
        try:
            tags_tuple = tuple(str(t) for t in tags)
            if sum(len(t) for t in tags_tuple) <= 4096:
                cache_key = (tags_tuple, bool(local_only))
                with _GUARD_CACHE_LOCK:
                    cached = _TAGS_CACHE.get(cache_key)
                    if cached is not None:
                        _TAGS_CACHE.move_to_end(cache_key)
                        return list(cached[0]), cached[1], cached[2]
            else:
                tags_tuple = None
        except Exception:
            tags_tuple = None
            cache_key = None

    clean_tags: list[str] = []
    final_local_only = bool(local_only)
    warnings: list[str] = []

    for raw_tag in tags:
        t = _CONTROL_CHAR_RE.sub("", str(raw_tag or "")).strip()
        if not t:
            continue
        if len(t) > 128:
            raise ValueError("Individual tag length cannot exceed 128 characters.")
        if is_poison_attempt(t):
            raise ValueError(
                f"Prompt injection or adversarial instruction pattern detected in tag '{t[:40]}'."
            )
        if is_credential(t):
            t = redact_secrets_local(t)
            final_local_only = True
            warnings.append("Credential pattern detected in tag: redacted and forced to private (local_only).")
        if t not in clean_tags:
            clean_tags.append(t)

    warnings_tuple = tuple(warnings)
    if cache_key is not None:
        with _GUARD_CACHE_LOCK:
            if len(_TAGS_CACHE) >= _MAX_GUARD_CACHE:
                _TAGS_CACHE.popitem(last=False)
            _TAGS_CACHE[cache_key] = (list(clean_tags), final_local_only, warnings_tuple)

    return clean_tags, final_local_only, warnings_tuple
=== END FILE: mesh/guards.py ===
=== FILE: mesh/passive.py (LINES: 944) ===
"""
mesh/passive.py — Passive memory capture from agent session logs.

Reads a conversation log (raw text, file path, directory path, or JSONL)
and extracts facts worth storing in Mesh.

Addresses Findings:
  - S2, S3, S4, S10, N2: Delegates credential detection, local redaction, cloud redaction, and
                         poison detection to `mesh.secrets` and `mesh.guards`
  - S7: Never implicitly uploads session logs to cloud providers merely because `MESH_AUTO_APPROVE=1`
        and an ambient API key are present; requires explicit cloud provider opt-in or dedicated
        per-invocation consent (`MESH_ALLOW_CLOUD_CAPTURE=1` or interactive terminal confirmation)
  - S8: Tags passively captured memories with `trust_tier="log_captured"` and supports automatic
        quarantine (`quarantine=True` when `MESH_QUARANTINE_CAPTURE=1` or `quarantine=True` is passed)
  - N1: Confines `_resolve_log_input` to non-symlinked `.log`/`.txt`/`.jsonl`/`.md` files and
        bounded directory scans (skipping hidden/dot directories like `.ssh`, `.aws`, `.gnupg`)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Optional

from . import ui
from .audit import reject_symlink, validate_namespace
from .envutil import env_bool
from .guards import (
    INVISIBLE_CHAR_CLASS,
    ZERO_WIDTH_RE,
    is_poison_attempt,
)
from .memory import VALID_MEMORY_TYPES, Mesh
from .secrets import (
    is_credential,
    redact_secrets_for_cloud,
    redact_secrets_local,
)

# Backwards-compatible aliases for internal callers and existing test suites
_INVISIBLE_CHAR_CLASS = INVISIBLE_CHAR_CLASS
_ZERO_WIDTH_RE = ZERO_WIDTH_RE
_is_credential = is_credential
_is_poison_attempt = is_poison_attempt
_redact_secrets_for_cloud = redact_secrets_for_cloud
_redact_secrets_local = redact_secrets_local

EXTRACTION_PROMPT = """You are a memory extraction assistant for Mesh, a shared agent memory system.
Given a conversation or session log between a human and an AI agent, extract facts, decisions,
processes, instructions, preferences, and discoveries that would be valuable to remember for future sessions.

Rules:
- Only extract concrete, specific, reusable facts
- Do NOT extract opinions, casual conversation, or things only relevant in the moment
- Do NOT extract raw passwords, API keys, or secrets in plain text without marking them private=true
- Each memory should be a single, self-contained sentence
- Prefer specifics over generalities: "the deploy script is at ./scripts/deploy.sh" not "there is a deploy script"

Return ONLY a JSON array of objects. No preamble, no explanation, no markdown fences.
Example:
  [{"content": "the staging server URL is staging.example.com", "type": "fact", "tags": ["infrastructure"], "private": false},
   {"content": "always run tests before merging to main", "type": "instruction", "tags": ["workflow"], "private": false},
   {"content": "API key is [REDACTED_SECRET]", "type": "fact", "tags": ["credentials"], "private": true}]

Valid types: fact, preference, context, result, instruction, process, decision, error, general
If nothing worth remembering was found, return an empty array: []

Session log to analyze:
"""

_TYPE_ALIASES = {
    "rule": "instruction",
    "guideline": "instruction",
    "workflow": "process",
    "choice": "decision",
    "bug": "error",
    "fix": "result",
    "outcome": "result",
    "background": "context",
    "setting": "preference",
    "note": "general",
}

_LOG_FILE_EXTENSIONS = {".log", ".txt", ".jsonl", ".md"}
_FORBIDDEN_FILENAMES = {
    ".env", ".env.local", ".env.production", ".netrc", ".pgpass",
    "id_rsa", "id_ed25519", "id_ecdsa", "authorized_keys", "known_hosts",
    "credentials", "config", "shadow", "passwd", "master.key", "auth_token",
}
_FORBIDDEN_DIRS = {
    ".ssh", ".aws", ".gnupg", ".kube", ".docker", ".config", ".git", ".mesh",
}


def _normalize_memory_type(raw_type: Optional[str]) -> str:
    """Ensure any LLM- or heuristic-generated memory type maps cleanly to VALID_MEMORY_TYPES."""
    if not raw_type:
        return "fact"
    cleaned = str(raw_type).strip().lower()
    if cleaned in VALID_MEMORY_TYPES:
        return cleaned
    return _TYPE_ALIASES.get(cleaned, "general")


def _is_safe_log_file_path(path: Path) -> bool:
    """
    Finding N1: Verify that `path` is a non-symlinked log file with an allowed extension
    (`.log`, `.txt`, `.jsonl`, `.md`) and does not reside inside sensitive dot-directories
    (`.ssh`, `.aws`, `.gnupg`, etc.).
    """
    try:
        if path.is_symlink():
            return False
        name_low = path.name.lower()
        if name_low in _FORBIDDEN_FILENAMES or name_low.startswith(".env"):
            return False
        if path.suffix.lower() not in _LOG_FILE_EXTENSIONS:
            return False
        for part in path.parts:
            if part.lower() in _FORBIDDEN_DIRS:
                return False
        return path.is_file()
    except OSError:
        return False


def _resolve_log_input(log_input: str, max_chars: int = 16000) -> str:
    """
    Accept either raw log text OR a verified log file/directory path string.
    Finding N1: Refuses symlinks, non-log file extensions (e.g. `~/.ssh/config`, `.env`),
    and bounds directory traversal depth and file count.
    """
    if not log_input or not log_input.strip():
        return ""
    stripped = log_input.strip()
    if "\n" not in stripped and len(stripped) < 1024:
        try:
            candidate = Path(stripped).expanduser()
            if not candidate.is_symlink() and candidate.exists():
                if candidate.is_file():
                    if _is_safe_log_file_path(candidate):
                        return _read_log_file(candidate, max_chars=max_chars)
                    return stripped
                if candidate.is_dir():
                    files: list[tuple[int, Path]] = []
                    inspected = 0
                    base_depth = len(candidate.resolve().parts)
                    for root, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(candidate, followlinks=False):
                        root_path = Path(root)
                        if len(root_path.parts) - base_depth > 3:
                            dirnames[:] = []
                            continue
                        dirnames[:] = [
                            d
                            for d in dirnames
                            if not d.startswith(".") and d.lower() not in _FORBIDDEN_DIRS
                        ]
                        for fname in filenames:
                            inspected += 1
                            if inspected > 64:
                                break
                            child = root_path / fname
                            if _is_safe_log_file_path(child):
                                try:
                                    files.append((child.stat().st_mtime_ns, child))
                                except OSError:
                                    continue
                        if inspected > 64:
                            break
                    files.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
                    chunks: list[str] = []
                    total_len = 0
                    for _, fpath in files[:8]:
                        chunk = _read_log_file(fpath, max_chars=max_chars // 2)
                        if chunk:
                            chunks.append(chunk)
                            total_len += len(chunk)
                            if total_len >= max_chars:
                                break
                    chunks.reverse()
                    return "\n\n".join(chunks)
        except OSError:
            pass
    return stripped


def _jsonl_record_text(value: Any) -> str:
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, list):
        parts = [_jsonl_record_text(item) for item in value]
        return "\n".join(part for part in parts if part.strip())
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        for key in ("text", "content", "message"):
            if key in value:
                text = _jsonl_record_text(value[key])
                if text.strip():
                    return text
    return ""


def _read_log_file(path: Path, max_chars: int = 16000) -> str:
    """Read text from a verified log or JSONL file, keeping the tail if the file is very large."""
    try:
        reject_symlink(path)
        if not _is_safe_log_file_path(path):
            return ""
        size = path.stat().st_size
        with open(path, "rb") as f:
            if size > max_chars * 4:
                f.seek(max(0, size - max_chars * 4))
            raw = f.read().decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
        if path.suffix.lower() == ".jsonl":
            extracted_lines: list[str] = []
            for line in raw.splitlines():
                line = line.strip()
                if not line:
                    continue
                try:
                    obj = json.loads(line)
                    if isinstance(obj, dict):
                        text = _jsonl_record_text(obj)
                        if text.strip():
                            extracted_lines.append(text.strip())
                            continue
                    elif isinstance(obj, str) and obj.strip():
                        extracted_lines.append(obj.strip())
                        continue
                except Exception:
                    pass
                extracted_lines.append(line)
            return "\n".join(extracted_lines)[-max_chars:]
        return raw[-max_chars:]
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return ""


def detect_provider(*, allow_implicit: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Detect the AI provider configuration.
    Finding S7: When `allow_implicit=False` (or when `MESH_NO_IMPLICIT_CLOUD=1` is set),
    ambient `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` / `OPENAI_API_KEY` / `GROQ_API_KEY` environment variables are
    ignored unless `MESH_AI_PROVIDER` (or `MESH_AI_API_KEY` + `MESH_AI_BASE_URL`) is explicitly set.
    """
    explicit = os.environ.get("MESH_AI_PROVIDER", "").lower().strip()
    api_key_override = os.environ.get("MESH_AI_API_KEY", "").strip()
    model_override = os.environ.get("MESH_AI_MODEL", "").strip()
    base_url_override = os.environ.get("MESH_AI_BASE_URL", "").strip()

    if api_key_override and base_url_override:
        return {
            "provider": "openai-compatible",
            "api_key": api_key_override,
            "model": model_override or "gpt-4o-mini",
            "base_url": base_url_override,
            "available": True,
            "explicit": True,
        }

    if explicit == "anthropic":
        key = api_key_override or os.environ.get("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "")
        return {
            "provider": "anthropic",
            "api_key": key,
            "model": model_override or "claude-haiku-4-5-20251001",
            "base_url": None,
            "available": bool(key),
            "explicit": True,
        }
    if explicit in ("openai",):
        key = api_key_override or os.environ.get("OPENAI_API_KEY", "")
        return {
            "provider": "openai",
            "api_key": key,
            "model": model_override or "gpt-4o-mini",
            "base_url": base_url_override or "https://api.openai.com/v1",
            "available": bool(key),
            "explicit": True,
        }
    if explicit == "groq":
        key = api_key_override or os.environ.get("GROQ_API_KEY", "")
        return {
            "provider": "openai-compatible",
            "api_key": key,
            "model": model_override or "llama3-8b-8192",
            "base_url": base_url_override or "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1",
            "available": bool(key),
            "explicit": True,
        }
    if explicit == "ollama":
        base = base_url_override or "http://localhost:11434/v1"
        return {
            "provider": "openai-compatible",
            "api_key": "ollama",
            "model": model_override or "llama3",
            "base_url": base,
            "available": _ollama_is_running(base),
            "explicit": True,
        }
    if explicit == "openai-compatible":
        key = api_key_override or ""
        base = base_url_override or ""
        return {
            "provider": "openai-compatible",
            "api_key": key,
            "model": model_override or "gpt-4o-mini",
            "base_url": base,
            "available": bool(key and base),
            "explicit": True,
        }
    if explicit == "local":
        return {
            "provider": "local",
            "api_key": None,
            "model": None,
            "base_url": None,
            "available": True,
            "explicit": True,
        }

    # Finding S7: Do not implicitly select cloud providers when disabled or when MESH_AUTO_APPROVE=1
    # is set without an explicit MESH_AI_PROVIDER (prevents silent background log exfiltration).
    if not allow_implicit or env_bool("MESH_NO_IMPLICIT_CLOUD", False) or env_bool("MESH_AUTO_APPROVE", False):
        return {
            "provider": "local",
            "api_key": None,
            "model": None,
            "base_url": None,
            "available": True,
            "explicit": False,
        }

    anthropic_key = os.environ.get("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "")
    if anthropic_key and not api_key_override:
        return {
            "provider": "anthropic",
            "api_key": anthropic_key,
            "model": model_override or "claude-haiku-4-5-20251001",
            "base_url": None,
            "available": True,
            "explicit": False,
        }

    openai_key = os.environ.get("OPENAI_API_KEY", "")
    if openai_key and not api_key_override:
        return {
            "provider": "openai-compatible",
            "api_key": openai_key,
            "model": model_override or "gpt-4o-mini",
            "base_url": "https://api.openai.com/v1",
            "available": True,
            "explicit": False,
        }

    groq_key = os.environ.get("GROQ_API_KEY", "")
    if groq_key and not api_key_override:
        return {
            "provider": "openai-compatible",
            "api_key": groq_key,
            "model": model_override or "llama3-8b-8192",
            "base_url": "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1",
            "available": True,
            "explicit": False,
        }

    ollama_base = base_url_override or "http://localhost:11434/v1"
    if _ollama_is_running(ollama_base):
        return {
            "provider": "openai-compatible",
            "api_key": "ollama",
            "model": model_override or _detect_ollama_model(ollama_base),
            "base_url": ollama_base,
            "available": True,
            "explicit": False,
        }

    if api_key_override:
        return {
            "provider": "openai-compatible",
            "api_key": api_key_override,
            "model": model_override or "gpt-4o-mini",
            "base_url": base_url_override or "https://api.openai.com/v1",
            "available": True,
            "explicit": False,
        }

    return {
        "provider": "local",
        "api_key": None,
        "model": None,
        "base_url": None,
        "available": True,
        "explicit": False,
    }


def _request_cloud_upload_consent(provider_name: str, assume_yes: bool = False) -> bool:
    """
    Finding S7 / 2g: Cloud log upload consent that `MESH_AUTO_APPROVE=1` alone CANNOT grant.
    Requires:
      1. `assume_yes=True` (explicit per-invocation parameter/flag), OR
      2. `MESH_ALLOW_CLOUD_CAPTURE=1` (dedicated cloud upload consent env var), OR
      3. Interactive TTY confirmation at the keyboard (with `MESH_AUTO_APPROVE` temporarily ignored).
    """
    if assume_yes or env_bool("MESH_ALLOW_CLOUD_CAPTURE", False):
        return True
    if env_bool("MESH_NON_INTERACTIVE", False) or not ui.interactive():
        return False
    prev_auto = os.environ.pop("MESH_AUTO_APPROVE", None)
    try:
        return ui.confirm(
            f"Allow Mesh to send redacted session log text to external cloud LLM provider '{provider_name}' for memory extraction?",
            default=False,
            assume_yes=False,
        )
    finally:
        if prev_auto is not None:
            os.environ["MESH_AUTO_APPROVE"] = prev_auto


def _ollama_is_running(base_url: str) -> bool:
    try:
        import httpx

        r = httpx.get(base_url.replace("/v1", ""), timeout=1.5)
        return r.status_code < 500
    except Exception:
        return False


def _detect_ollama_model(base_url: str) -> str:
    try:
        import httpx

        r = httpx.get(base_url.replace("/v1", "/api/tags"), timeout=2.0)
        if r.status_code == 200:
            models = r.json().get("models", [])
            if models:
                return models[0]["name"]
    except Exception:
        pass
    return "llama3"


def _extract_with_anthropic(log_text: str, config: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    import httpx

    safe_log_text = redact_secrets_for_cloud(log_text)
    response = httpx.post(
        "https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages",
        headers={
            "x-api-key": config["api_key"],
            "anthropic-version": "2023-06-01",
            "content-type": "application/json",
        },
        json={
            "model": config["model"],
            "max_tokens": 1000,
            "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": EXTRACTION_PROMPT + safe_log_text}],
        },
        timeout=30,
    )
    if response.status_code == 401:
        raise ValueError("Invalid Anthropic API key. Check ANTHROPIC_API_KEY.")
    if response.status_code == 429:
        raise ValueError("Anthropic rate limit hit. Try again in a moment.")
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise ValueError(f"Anthropic API error {response.status_code}: {response.text[:200]}")
    raw = response.json()["content"][0]["text"].strip()
    return _parse_json_response(raw)


def _extract_with_openai_compatible(log_text: str, config: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    import httpx

    safe_log_text = redact_secrets_for_cloud(log_text)
    base_url = (config.get("base_url") or "https://api.openai.com/v1").rstrip("/")
    headers = {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "Authorization": f"Bearer {config['api_key']}",
    }
    response = httpx.post(
        f"{base_url}/chat/completions",
        headers=headers,
        json={
            "model": config["model"],
            "max_tokens": 1000,
            "temperature": 0.2,
            "messages": [
                {
                    "role": "system",
                    "content": "You are a memory extraction assistant. Always respond with valid JSON only.",
                },
                {"role": "user", "content": EXTRACTION_PROMPT + safe_log_text},
            ],
        },
        timeout=30,
    )
    if response.status_code == 401:
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid API key for {base_url}. Check your MESH_AI_API_KEY.")
    if response.status_code == 429:
        raise ValueError("Rate limit hit. Try again in a moment.")
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise ValueError(f"API error {response.status_code} from {base_url}: {response.text[:200]}")
    raw = response.json()["choices"][0]["message"]["content"].strip()
    return _parse_json_response(raw)


_SIGNAL_PATTERNS = [
    r"\bis at\b",
    r"\bis located at\b",
    r"\brunning on\b",
    r"\bport\s+\d+",
    r"\bURL is\b",
    r"\bpath is\b",
    r"\bscript is\b",
    r"\bcommand is\b",
    r"\balways\b",
    r"\bnever\b",
    r"\bdon't\b",
    r"\bdo not\b",
    r"\bmust\b",
    r"\bshould\b",
    r"\bwe decided\b",
    r"\bdecided to\b",
    r"\bwe use\b",
    r"\bwe're using\b",
    r"\bconfigured to\b",
    r"\bset to\b",
    r"\bdeployed on\b",
    r"\bstored in\b",
    r"\bprefers?\b",
    r"\bcredential",
    r"\bpassword\b",
    r"\bapi key\b",
    r"\btoken\b",
    r"\bsecret\b",
]

_NOISE_PATTERNS = [
    r"^\s*(yes|no|ok|okay|sure|thanks|thank you|great|got it|understood|sounds good)\s*[.!]?\s*$",
    r"^\s*okay\s+sounds\s+good\b",
    r"^\s*i\s+(see|understand|think|feel|wonder|believe)\b",
    r"^\s*(can|could|would|should)\s+you\b",
    r"^\s*please\b",
    r"^\s*here\s+(is|are)\b",
    r"^\s*let me\b",
    r"^\s*i'll\b",
]

_compiled_signals = [re.compile(p, re.IGNORECASE) for p in _SIGNAL_PATTERNS]
_compiled_noise = [re.compile(p, re.IGNORECASE) for p in _NOISE_PATTERNS]


def _is_noise(sentence: str) -> bool:
    return any(p.match(sentence) for p in _compiled_noise)


def _has_signal(sentence: str) -> bool:
    return any(p.search(sentence) for p in _compiled_signals)


def _infer_type(sentence: str) -> str:
    s = sentence.lower()
    if any(w in s for w in ["decided", "decision", "chose", "going with", "we'll use"]):
        return "decision"
    if any(w in s for w in ["prefer", "prefers", "preference"]):
        return "preference"
    if any(w in s for w in ["always", "never", "must", "should", "don't", "do not", "process", "workflow"]):
        return "process"
    if any(w in s for w in ["error", "bug", "issue", "problem", "broken", "fails", "crash"]):
        return "error"
    return "fact"


def _extract_local(log_text: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    sentences = re.split(r"(?<=[.!?])\s+|\n", log_text)
    results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for raw in sentences:
        sentence = raw.strip()
        if len(sentence) < 20 or len(sentence) > 300:
            continue
        if _is_noise(sentence) or is_poison_attempt(sentence):
            continue
        key = sentence.lower()[:60]
        if key in seen:
            continue
        if _has_signal(sentence):
            is_private = is_credential(sentence)
            if is_private:
                sentence = redact_secrets_local(sentence)
            results.append(
                {
                    "content": sentence,
                    "type": _infer_type(sentence),
                    "tags": ["auto-captured", "local-extraction"],
                    "private": is_private,
                }
            )
            seen.add(key)
        if len(results) >= 15:
            break
    return results


def _parse_json_response(raw: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    if "```" in raw:
        parts = raw.split("```")
        for part in parts:
            part = part.strip()
            if part.startswith("json"):
                part = part[4:].strip()
            if part.startswith("["):
                raw = part
                break
    raw = raw.strip()
    if not raw.startswith("["):
        match = re.search(r"\[.*\]", raw, re.DOTALL)
        if match:
            raw = match.group(0)
        else:
            return []
    parsed = json.loads(raw)
    if not isinstance(parsed, list):
        return []
    return [item for item in parsed if isinstance(item, dict)]


def extract_memories_from_log(
    log_text: str,
    namespace: str = "shared",
    agent_id: str = "passive-capture",
    dry_run: bool = False,
    verbose: bool = False,
    force_local: bool = False,
    assume_yes: bool = False,
    default_local_only: bool = False,
    resolve_input: bool = True,
    quarantine: Optional[bool] = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Extract and store memories from a session log (or verified log file/directory path).
    Finding S7: Requires explicit cloud consent (`assume_yes=True`, `MESH_ALLOW_CLOUD_CAPTURE=1`,
    or interactive TTY prompt) that `MESH_AUTO_APPROVE=1` alone cannot grant.
    Finding S8: Marks captured memories with `trust_tier="log_captured"` and supports quarantine.
    """
    clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    resolved_text = _resolve_log_input(log_text) if resolve_input else str(log_text or "")
    if not resolved_text.strip():
        return {
            "captured_count": 0,
            "stored_count": 0,
            "deduplicated_count": 0,
            "skipped_count": 0,
            "provider_used": "none",
            "memories": [],
            "error": None,
        }

    if len(resolved_text) > 8000:
        resolved_text = "[earlier content truncated]\n\n" + resolved_text[-8000:]

    if force_local:
        config: dict[str, Any] = {"provider": "local", "available": True}
    else:
        config = detect_provider()

    if config["provider"] != "local" and not config.get("available"):
        if verbose:
            ui.info(
                f"Configured provider '{config['provider']}' is unavailable — using local keyword extraction"
            )
        config = {"provider": "local", "available": True}

    # Finding S7: Require dedicated cloud consent (`MESH_AUTO_APPROVE` alone does not grant cloud upload)
    if config["provider"] != "local":
        provider_name = config["provider"]
        if not _request_cloud_upload_consent(provider_name, assume_yes=assume_yes):
            if verbose:
                ui.info("Cloud extraction declined — using local keyword extraction instead")
            config = {"provider": "local", "available": True}

    if verbose:
        if config["provider"] == "local":
            ui.info("Using local keyword extraction", "No cloud AI calls are made.")
        else:
            provider_label = config["provider"]
            model_label = config.get("model", "")
            base_label = f" ({config['base_url']})" if config.get("base_url") else ""
            ui.info(f"Using {provider_label} / {model_label}", base_label.strip(" ()") or None)

    extracted: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    extraction_error: Optional[str] = None
    try:
        if config["provider"] == "anthropic":
            extracted = _extract_with_anthropic(resolved_text, config)
            provider_used = f"anthropic/{config['model']}"
        elif config["provider"] in ("openai", "openai-compatible"):
            extracted = _extract_with_openai_compatible(resolved_text, config)
            base = config.get("base_url", "")
            provider_used = f"openai-compatible/{config['model']} ({base})"
        else:
            extracted = _extract_local(resolved_text)
            provider_used = "local-keyword-extractor"
    except Exception as e:
        extraction_error = str(e)
        if verbose:
            ui.warn(f"AI extraction failed: {e}", "Falling back to local keyword extraction.")
        try:
            extracted = _extract_local(resolved_text)
            provider_used = "local-keyword-extractor (fallback)"
        except Exception as fallback_err:
            if verbose:
                ui.error(f"Local fallback also failed: {fallback_err}")
            return {
                "captured_count": 0,
                "stored_count": 0,
                "deduplicated_count": 0,
                "skipped_count": 0,
                "provider_used": "none",
                "memories": [],
                "error": extraction_error,
            }

    if not extracted:
        if verbose:
            ui.info("Nothing worth remembering was found in that log")
        return {
            "captured_count": 0,
            "stored_count": 0,
            "deduplicated_count": 0,
            "skipped_count": 0,
            "provider_used": provider_used,
            "memories": [],
            "error": extraction_error,
        }

    if dry_run:
        ui.header("Capture preview", clean_ns, "dry run")
        ui.kv([("Would store", ui.plural(len(extracted), "memory", "memories")), ("Provider", str(provider_used))])
        print()
        for mem in extracted:
            preview_content = str(mem.get("content", ""))
            private = (
                default_local_only
                or bool(mem.get("private"))
                or is_credential(preview_content)
            )
            private_flag = "  " + ui.badge("private", "accent") if private else ""
            mtype = _normalize_memory_type(mem.get("type"))
            print(f"{ui.IND}{ui.style(ui.sym('bullet'), 'muted')} {ui.style(mtype, 'info')}{private_flag}  {' '.join(str(mem.get('content', '')).split())}")
        print()
        ui.hint("Nothing was written. Re-run without --dry-run to store these.", stream=sys.stdout)
        return {
            "captured_count": len(extracted),
            "stored_count": 0,
            "deduplicated_count": 0,
            "skipped_count": len(extracted),
            "provider_used": provider_used,
            "memories": extracted,
            "error": extraction_error,
        }

    should_quarantine = (
        env_bool("MESH_QUARANTINE_CAPTURE", False)
        if quarantine is None
        else bool(quarantine)
    )

    mesh = Mesh(namespace=clean_ns, agent_id=agent_id)
    captured = 0
    stored = 0
    deduplicated = 0
    skipped = 0
    for mem in extracted:
        content = str(mem.get("content") or "").strip()
        if not content:
            skipped += 1
            continue
        if is_poison_attempt(content):
            skipped += 1
            if verbose:
                ui.warn(f"Ignored suspicious content: {ui.truncate(content, 60)}")
            continue
        try:
            mtype = _normalize_memory_type(mem.get("type", "general"))
            raw_tags = mem.get("tags") or []
            if not isinstance(raw_tags, list):
                raw_tags = [str(raw_tags)]
            tags = list(dict.fromkeys([*(str(t) for t in raw_tags), "auto-captured"]))
            has_credential = is_credential(content)
            is_private = (
                default_local_only
                or bool(mem.get("private", False))
                or has_credential
            )
            # Finding S10: Use local redaction so non-secret emails are preserved in local DB
            if has_credential:
                content = redact_secrets_local(content)
            learn_res = mesh.learn(
                content=content,
                memory_type=mtype,
                tags=tags,
                local_only=is_private,
                confidence=0.8,
                trust_tier="log_captured",
                quarantined=should_quarantine,
            )
            captured += 1
            if learn_res.get("deduplicated"):
                deduplicated += 1
            else:
                stored += 1
            if verbose:
                private_flag = "  " + ui.badge("private", "accent") if is_private else ""
                ui.success(f"{ui.truncate(content, 80)}{private_flag}")
        except Exception as e:
            skipped += 1
            if extraction_error is None:
                extraction_error = str(e)
            if verbose:
                ui.error(f"Couldn't store \"{ui.truncate(content, 60)}\": {e}")

    return {
        "captured_count": captured,
        "stored_count": stored,
        "deduplicated_count": deduplicated,
        "skipped_count": skipped,
        "provider_used": provider_used,
        "memories": extracted,
        "quarantined": should_quarantine,
        "error": extraction_error,
    }


def capture_cmd() -> None:
    """Entry point for: mesh-capture"""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Extract and store memories from a session log automatically.",
        epilog=""" Examples:   mesh-capture --file session.log   mesh-capture --file session.log --dry-run --verbose   cat output.txt | mesh-capture --stdin   mesh-capture --file session.log --local-only        """,
    )
    parser.add_argument("--file", help="Path to session log file or directory")
    parser.add_argument("--stdin", action="store_true", help="Read log from stdin")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--namespace",
        default=None,
        help="Mesh namespace (default: MESH_NAMESPACE env var or 'shared')",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--dry-run",
        action="store_true",
        help="Preview what would be stored without writing anything",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--verbose",
        action="store_true",
        help="Show detailed progress and provider info",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--local-only",
        action="store_true",
        help="Force local keyword extraction, skip all AI providers",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--quarantine",
        action="store_true",
        help="Store captured memories in quarantine until approved with `mesh approve`",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--show-provider",
        action="store_true",
        help="Print which provider would be used and exit",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--yes", "-y", action="store_true", help="Skip interactive y/n permission prompts")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if args.show_provider:
        config = detect_provider()
        if config["provider"] == "local":
            ui.header("Capture provider")
            print()
            ui.kv([("Provider", "local keyword extractor"), ("AI key", ui.style("none found", "muted"))])
            ui.hint("For AI extraction set MESH_AI_PROVIDER and ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, OPENAI_API_KEY or GROQ_API_KEY.", stream=sys.stdout)
        else:
            ui.header("Capture provider")
            print()
            ui.kv(
                [
                    ("Provider", str(config["provider"])),
                    ("Model", str(config.get("model", "")) or None),
                    ("Base URL", str(config["base_url"]) if config.get("base_url") else None),
                ]
            )
        return

    if not args.file and not args.stdin:
        parser.print_help()
        ui.error("Tell Mesh where to read from", "Use --file <path> or pipe a log in with --stdin.")
        sys.exit(1)

    if args.stdin:
        log_text = sys.stdin.read(1_000_000)
    else:
        path = Path(args.file).expanduser()
        if not path.exists():
            ui.fail(f"File not found: {args.file}", "Check the path, or pipe the log in with --stdin.")
        if not path.is_dir() and not _is_safe_log_file_path(path):
            ui.fail(
                f"Refusing to read '{args.file}': only non-symlinked .log, .txt, .jsonl, and .md files are allowed."
            )
        log_text = _resolve_log_input(str(path))

    ns = validate_namespace(args.namespace or os.environ.get("MESH_NAMESPACE", "shared"))
    result = extract_memories_from_log(
        log_text=log_text,
        namespace=ns,
        dry_run=args.dry_run,
        verbose=args.verbose,
        force_local=args.local_only,
        assume_yes=args.yes,
        resolve_input=False,
        quarantine=True if args.quarantine else None,
    )
    if not args.dry_run:
        provider_label = result.get("provider_used", "unknown")
        dedup_cnt = int(result.get("deduplicated_count", 0))
        detail_parts = [f"{result['skipped_count']} skipped"]
        if dedup_cnt:
            detail_parts.append(f"{dedup_cnt} deduplicated")
        detail_parts.append(f"provider: {provider_label}")
        detail_str = f"  {ui.sym('dot')}  ".join(detail_parts)
        print()
        if result["stored_count"] or dedup_cnt:
            ui.success(
                f"Captured {ui.plural(result['stored_count'], 'new memory', 'new memories')} into {ns}",
                detail_str,
            )
            ui.hint("See them with `mesh inspect`", stream=sys.stdout)
        else:
            ui.info("Nothing was stored", detail_str)

=== FILE: mesh/skill_installer.py (LINES: 417) ===
"""
mesh/skill_installer.py — Agent skill installer for mesh-context-layer.

Copies the bundled mesh-context-layer SKILL.md (and any sibling files) into
every AI coding-agent skill folder detected on this machine.

Supported agents (verified global skill directories):
  - Antigravity / Gemini CLI IDE   ~/.gemini/config/skills/
  - Gemini CLI                     ~/.gemini/skills/
  - Claude Code                    ~/.claude/skills/
  - Cursor                         ~/.cursor/skills/
                                   %APPDATA%/Cursor/User/skills/ (Win fallback)
                                   ~/Library/Application Support/Cursor/User/skills/ (macOS fallback)
                                   ~/.config/cursor/User/skills/ (Linux fallback)
  - OpenAI Codex CLI               ~/.codex/skills/
  - OpenCode                       ~/.config/opencode/skills/
  - Windsurf (Cascade)             ~/.codeium/windsurf/skills/
  - Generic .agents standard       ~/.agents/skills/
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import shutil
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable

from .version import VERSION

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Bundled skill source — lives next to this file inside the installed package
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_SKILL_NAME = "mesh-context-layer"
_SKILLS_DIR = Path(__file__).parent / "skills"
_VERSION_MARKER_FILE = ".mesh-skill-version"


def _skill_source() -> Path:
    """Return the path to the bundled mesh-context-layer skill directory."""
    src = _SKILLS_DIR / _SKILL_NAME
    if not src.is_dir():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Bundled skill directory not found: {src}\n"
            "This usually means mesh-context-layer was not installed correctly.\n"
            "Try: pip install --force-reinstall mesh-context-layer"
        )
    return src


def _is_symlink_or_junction(path: Path) -> bool:
    """Return True if `path` is a POSIX/Windows symlink or a Windows NTFS junction."""
    if path.is_symlink():
        return True
    is_junc = getattr(path, "is_junction", None)
    if is_junc is not None:
        try:
            if is_junc():  # Python 3.12+
                return True
        except OSError:
            pass
    if os.name == "nt":
        try:
            st = path.lstat()
            # FILE_ATTRIBUTE_REPARSE_POINT == 0x400
            if getattr(st, "st_file_attributes", 0) & 0x400:
                return True
        except OSError:
            pass
    return False


def _remove_path_or_link(path: Path) -> None:
    """Safely remove a directory, symlink, or Windows junction."""
    if _is_symlink_or_junction(path):
        try:
            path.unlink()
            return
        except OSError:
            if os.name == "nt":
                os.rmdir(path)
                return
            raise
    if path.is_dir():
        shutil.rmtree(path)
    elif path.exists():
        path.unlink()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Agent skill directory resolution
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _candidate_skill_roots() -> list[tuple[str, Path]]:
    """
    Return a deduplicated list of `(agent_label, skills_root_path)` tuples for
    every AI coding agent detected on this machine. Only includes directories
    whose *parent* already exists (we don't create new `~/.config/` trees).
    """
    home = Path.home()
    appdata_raw = os.environ.get("APPDATA", "").strip() if os.name == "nt" else ""
    appdata = Path(appdata_raw) if appdata_raw else None
    xdg_raw = os.environ.get("XDG_CONFIG_HOME", "").strip()
    xdg_config = Path(xdg_raw) if xdg_raw else (home / ".config")

    candidates: list[tuple[str, Path]] = [
        # ── Antigravity (Global scope: ~/.gemini/config/skills/)
        # Verified: https://antigravity.google/docs/skills/
        #           https://codelabs.developers.google.com/getting-started-with-antigravity-skills
        ("Antigravity", home / ".gemini" / "config" / "skills"),

        # ── Gemini CLI (Global scope: ~/.gemini/skills/)
        # Verified: https://geminicli.com/docs/cli/tutorials/skills-getting-started/
        ("Gemini CLI", home / ".gemini" / "skills"),

        # ── Claude Code (Global scope: ~/.claude/skills/)
        # Verified: https://docs.anthropic.com/en/docs/claude-code/skills
        ("Claude Code", home / ".claude" / "skills"),

        # ── Cursor (Global scope: ~/.cursor/skills/ + OS User/skills paths)
        # Verified: https://cursor.com/docs/skills
        ("Cursor", home / ".cursor" / "skills"),
        *(
            # Note: APPDATA on Windows already points to ...\AppData\Roaming
            [("Cursor", appdata / "Cursor" / "User" / "skills")]
            if appdata is not None
            else []
        ),
        *(
            [("Cursor", home / "Library" / "Application Support" / "Cursor" / "User" / "skills")]
            if sys.platform == "darwin"
            else []
        ),
        *(
            [("Cursor", xdg_config / "cursor" / "User" / "skills")]
            if sys.platform.startswith("linux")
            else []
        ),

        # ── OpenAI Codex CLI (Global scope: ~/.codex/skills/)
        # Verified: https://skills-hub.ai/codex-skills
        ("Codex CLI", home / ".codex" / "skills"),

        # ── OpenCode (Global scope: ~/.config/opencode/skills/)
        # Verified: https://opencode.ai/docs/skills/
        ("OpenCode", xdg_config / "opencode" / "skills"),

        # ── Windsurf / Cascade (Global scope: ~/.codeium/windsurf/skills/)
        # Verified: https://www.ui-skills.com/agents/windsurf
        ("Windsurf (Cascade)", home / ".codeium" / "windsurf" / "skills"),

        # ── Generic .agents standard (Global scope: ~/.agents/skills/)
        # Verified: https://cursor.com/docs/skills & https://opencode.ai/docs/skills/
        ("Generic .agents standard", home / ".agents" / "skills"),
    ]

    # Resolve any symlinks / junctions so we don't double-install
    seen: set[Path] = set()
    result: list[tuple[str, Path]] = []
    for label, path in candidates:
        try:
            resolved = path.resolve()
        except Exception:
            resolved = path
        if resolved in seen:
            continue
        # Only include if the parent directory (or the path itself) already exists on disk
        if path.parent.exists() or path.exists() or _is_symlink_or_junction(path):
            seen.add(resolved)
            result.append((label, path))

    return result


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Core installer
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _is_already_up_to_date(dst: Path) -> bool:
    """Return True if `dst` has `.mesh-skill-version` matching `VERSION` and `SKILL.md` exists."""
    if not (dst.exists() or _is_symlink_or_junction(dst)):
        return False
    version_file = dst / _VERSION_MARKER_FILE
    skill_md = dst / "SKILL.md"
    if not (version_file.is_file() and skill_md.is_file()):
        return False
    try:
        installed_ver = version_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
        return installed_ver == VERSION
    except OSError:
        return False


def _safe_replace_dir(src: Path, dst: Path, max_retries: int = 5, backoff: float = 0.05) -> None:
    """
    Atomically replace `dst` with `src` on Windows and POSIX.
    Handles transient [WinError 5] Access is denied during rapid directory renames
    caused by Windows file indexers, antivirus locks, or pending handle cleanup (Issue T-001).
    """
    for attempt in range(max_retries):
        try:
            os.replace(src, dst)
            return
        except (PermissionError, OSError):
            if attempt == max_retries - 1:
                raise
            import gc
            import time

            gc.collect()
            time.sleep(backoff * (2**attempt))


def install_skill(
    target_skills_root: Path,
    *,
    overwrite: bool = True,
    dry_run: bool = False,
    force: bool = False,
) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    """
    Atomically copy the bundled `mesh-context-layer` skill folder into `target_skills_root`:
      1. If `overwrite=False` and `dst` exists, skip with `"already installed"`.
      2. If `overwrite=True`, `force=False`, and `.mesh-skill-version == VERSION`, skip with `"already up to date"`.
      3. Copy to `dst.with_name(dst.name + ".tmp")`, write `.mesh-skill-version`, then atomically replace `dst`
         (unlinking `dst` first if it is a symlink or Windows junction so `rmtree` never fails).

    Returns `(status_message, was_changed)`.
    """
    src = _skill_source()
    dst = target_skills_root / _SKILL_NAME
    dst_exists = dst.exists() or _is_symlink_or_junction(dst)

    if dst_exists and not overwrite and not force:
        return f"already installed at {dst} (skipped)", False

    if dst_exists and not force and _is_already_up_to_date(dst):
        if dry_run:
            # A dry run changed nothing. Reporting True here made callers (and
            # `mesh setup`) claim a write that never happened.
            return f"would install to {dst} (already up to date v{VERSION})", False
        return f"already up to date (v{VERSION}) at {dst}", False

    if dry_run:
        return f"would install to {dst}", True

    dst.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    tmp_dst = dst.with_name(dst.name + ".tmp")
    backup_dst = dst.with_name(dst.name + ".bak")

    if tmp_dst.exists() or _is_symlink_or_junction(tmp_dst):
        _remove_path_or_link(tmp_dst)
    if backup_dst.exists() or _is_symlink_or_junction(backup_dst):
        _remove_path_or_link(backup_dst)

    try:
        shutil.copytree(src, tmp_dst)
        (tmp_dst / _VERSION_MARKER_FILE).write_text(f"{VERSION}\n", encoding="utf-8")

        if _is_symlink_or_junction(dst):
            _remove_path_or_link(dst)
            _safe_replace_dir(tmp_dst, dst)
        elif dst.exists():
            _safe_replace_dir(dst, backup_dst)
            try:
                _safe_replace_dir(tmp_dst, dst)
            except Exception:
                if backup_dst.exists() and not dst.exists():
                    _safe_replace_dir(backup_dst, dst)
                raise
            finally:
                if backup_dst.exists():
                    _remove_path_or_link(backup_dst)
        else:
            _safe_replace_dir(tmp_dst, dst)
    finally:
        if tmp_dst.exists() or _is_symlink_or_junction(tmp_dst):
            try:
                _remove_path_or_link(tmp_dst)
            except OSError:
                pass

    return f"installed -> {dst}", True


def install_all(
    *,
    assume_yes: bool = False,
    dry_run: bool = False,
    overwrite: bool = True,
    force: bool = False,
    permission_fn: Callable[[str, bool], bool] | None = None,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Detect all agent skill directories and install the skill into each one.
    Never prompts for permission when `dry_run=True` (Phase 4.10).

    Args:
        assume_yes: Skip interactive y/n prompts.
        dry_run: Preview what would happen without prompting or writing anything.
        overwrite: Overwrite an already-installed copy if outdated.
        force: Reinstall even when `.mesh-skill-version` equals `VERSION`.
        permission_fn: Optional `callable(description, assume_yes=...) -> bool`.
                       Defaults to `daemon.request_user_permission`.

    Returns:
        List of result dicts with keys: `agent`, `path`, `status`, `changed`.
    """
    candidates = _candidate_skill_roots()
    results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    if not candidates:
        results.append(
            {
                "agent": "—",
                "path": None,
                "status": "No agent skill directories detected on this machine.",
                "changed": False,
            }
        )
        return results

    perm_callable: Any
    if permission_fn is None:
        from .daemon import request_user_permission as default_perm_fn

        perm_callable = default_perm_fn
    else:
        perm_callable = permission_fn

    for label, skills_root in candidates:
        dst = skills_root / _SKILL_NAME
        dst_exists = dst.exists() or _is_symlink_or_junction(dst)

        if dst_exists and not overwrite and not force:
            results.append(
                {
                    "agent": label,
                    "path": str(skills_root),
                    "status": "already installed (skipped)",
                    "changed": False,
                }
            )
            continue

        if not dry_run and dst_exists and not force and _is_already_up_to_date(dst):
            results.append(
                {
                    "agent": label,
                    "path": str(skills_root),
                    "status": f"already up to date (v{VERSION})",
                    "changed": False,
                }
            )
            continue

        # dry_run=True must never prompt for permission (Phase 4.10)
        if not dry_run:
            action_verb = "update" if dst_exists else "install"
            prompt_desc = (
                f"{action_verb} the Mesh agent skill into {label} skill folder\n"
                f"      ({skills_root / _SKILL_NAME})"
            )
            try:
                approved = perm_callable(prompt_desc, assume_yes=assume_yes)
            except TypeError:
                approved = perm_callable(prompt_desc, assume_yes)

            if not approved:
                results.append(
                    {
                        "agent": label,
                        "path": str(skills_root),
                        "status": "skipped (user declined)",
                        "changed": False,
                    }
                )
                continue

        try:
            msg, changed = install_skill(
                skills_root,
                overwrite=overwrite,
                dry_run=dry_run,
                force=force,
            )
            results.append(
                {
                    "agent": label,
                    "path": str(skills_root),
                    "status": msg,
                    "changed": changed,
                }
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            results.append(
                {
                    "agent": label,
                    "path": str(skills_root),
                    "status": f"ERROR: {exc}",
                    "changed": False,
                }
            )

    return results


def get_installed_locations() -> list[Path]:
    """Return all paths where the mesh-context-layer skill is currently installed."""
    installed: list[Path] = []
    for _, skills_root in _candidate_skill_roots():
        dst = skills_root / _SKILL_NAME
        if dst.is_dir() and (dst / "SKILL.md").is_file():
            installed.append(dst)
    return installed
=== END FILE: mesh/skill_installer.py ===
=== FILE: mesh/store.py (LINES: 5796) ===
"""
mesh/store.py — High-performance unified persistence & hybrid search engine for Mesh.

Storage architecture:
  - SQLite (WAL mode) single-source-of-truth per namespace (`~/.mesh/mesh_<namespace>.db`)
  - Strict namespace (`validate_namespace`), memory ID (`validate_memory_id`), and symlink (`reject_symlink`) validation
  - Lowest-layer ingestion guard (`guard_ingest` & `guard_tags`) enforced on every write path
  - First-class provenance (`trust_tier`: 'user' | 'agent' | 'log_captured'), quarantine (`quarantined`),
    and secret locator (`secret_ref`) schema columns with automatic migration
  - Transparent at-rest encryption (`mesh.crypto`) for content, tags, embeddings, queries, and contradictions
  - FTS5 leakage defense: `local_only=True` and encrypted rows are never indexed into on-disk `memories_fts`
    shadow tables; hybrid search uses the in-memory decrypted lexical cache instead
  - Cross-process + in-process re-entrant `namespace_write_lock` (`~/.mesh/.mesh_<ns>.lock`, mode 0600)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import atexit
import contextlib
import json
import logging
import os
import re
import sqlite3
import stat
import sys
import threading
import time
import uuid
from collections import OrderedDict
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterator, Literal, Optional, Sequence

_VALID_MEMORY_ID_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,127}$")
_REDACTED_PLACEHOLDER_RE = re.compile(
    r"\[REDACTED_(?:SECRET|API_KEY|TOKEN|PASSWORD|JWT|PRIVATE_KEY|content|local_only)[^\]]*\]",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)

_PUNCT_SPLIT_RE = re.compile(r"[^a-z0-9]+")

class NamespaceNotFoundError(ValueError):
    """
    Raised when `MeshStore(namespace, create=False)` or `Mesh(namespace, create=False)`
    is instantiated for a namespace whose SQLite database file does not exist on disk.
    Inherits from `ValueError` for backwards compatibility with existing callers.
    """


class NamespaceLimitExceededError(ValueError):
    """
    Raised when creating a new namespace would exceed the configured namespace cap
    (`MESH_MAX_NAMESPACES`, default 256).
    """


def _normalize_trust_tier(raw_tier: Optional[str], default: str = "user") -> str:
    if not raw_tier:
        return default
    clean = str(raw_tier).strip().lower()
    return clean if clean in VALID_TRUST_TIERS else default


def _parse_secret_ref_json(raw_ref: Any) -> Optional[dict[str, str]]:
    if not raw_ref:
        return None
    try:
        plain = decrypt_text_if_encrypted(raw_ref) if isinstance(raw_ref, str) else raw_ref
        if isinstance(plain, dict):
            return {str(k): str(v) for k, v in plain.items()}
        if isinstance(plain, str) and plain.strip():
            parsed = json.loads(plain)
            if isinstance(parsed, dict):
                return {str(k): str(v) for k, v in parsed.items()}
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def _get_max_pool_size() -> int:
    if os.environ.get("MESH_MAX_POOL_SIZE"):
        return env_int("MESH_MAX_POOL_SIZE", _MAX_POOL_SIZE, minimum=2)
    if os.environ.get("MESH_MAX_OPEN_NAMESPACES"):
        return env_int("MESH_MAX_OPEN_NAMESPACES", _MAX_POOL_SIZE, minimum=2)
    return max(2, int(_MAX_POOL_SIZE))


def _get_max_vector_cache_size() -> int:
    if os.environ.get("MESH_MAX_VECTOR_CACHE_SIZE"):
        return env_int("MESH_MAX_VECTOR_CACHE_SIZE", _MAX_VECTOR_CACHE_SIZE, minimum=2)
    if os.environ.get("MESH_MAX_OPEN_NAMESPACES"):
        return env_int("MESH_MAX_OPEN_NAMESPACES", _MAX_VECTOR_CACHE_SIZE, minimum=2)
    return max(2, int(_MAX_VECTOR_CACHE_SIZE))


def _get_max_namespaces() -> int:
    if os.environ.get("MESH_MAX_NAMESPACES"):
        return env_int("MESH_MAX_NAMESPACES", _MAX_NAMESPACES, minimum=1)
    return max(1, int(_MAX_NAMESPACES))


def _check_namespace_creation_limit(base_dir: Path, namespace: str) -> None:
    """Enforce a cap on total tracked namespaces before creating a new `.db` file."""
    target_db = (base_dir / f"mesh_{namespace}.db").resolve()
    if target_db.exists():
        return
    max_ns = _get_max_namespaces()
    if not base_dir.exists():
        return
    existing_count = 0
    for p in base_dir.glob("mesh_*.db"):
        if p.is_file() and not p.name.endswith(("-wal", "-shm")):
            existing_count += 1
            if existing_count >= max_ns:
                raise NamespaceLimitExceededError(
                    f"Maximum number of tracked namespaces ({max_ns}) exceeded; "
                    f"refusing to create namespace '{namespace}'."
                )


def _evict_external_namespace_caches(namespace: str) -> None:
    """Evict cached state for `namespace` in audit, write-lock, context_builder, HTTP, MCP, and Dashboard."""
    evict_namespace_write_lock(namespace)
    evict_audit_cache(namespace)

    ctx_mod = sys.modules.get("mesh.context_builder")
    if ctx_mod is not None and hasattr(ctx_mod, "evict_context_mesh_cache"):
        try:
            ctx_mod.evict_context_mesh_cache(namespace)
        except Exception:
            pass

    http_mod = sys.modules.get("mesh.http_server")
    if http_mod is not None and hasattr(http_mod, "evict_http_mesh_cache"):
        try:
            http_mod.evict_http_mesh_cache(namespace)
        except Exception:
            pass

    mcp_mod = sys.modules.get("mesh.mcp_server")
    if mcp_mod is not None and hasattr(mcp_mod, "evict_mcp_mesh_cache"):
        try:
            mcp_mod.evict_mcp_mesh_cache(namespace)
        except Exception:
            pass

    dash_mod = sys.modules.get("mesh.dashboard")
    if dash_mod is not None and hasattr(dash_mod, "evict_dashboard_mesh_cache"):
        try:
            dash_mod.evict_dashboard_mesh_cache(namespace)
        except Exception:
            pass


def _finalize_evicted_resources(
    evicted: list[tuple[Path, Optional["_PooledConnection"], Optional[str]]]
) -> None:
    """
    Flush telemetry, close pooled connections, and evict upper-layer caches for
    LRU-evicted namespaces AFTER releasing `_CACHE_LOCK`.
    """
    for db_path, conn, ns in evicted:
        try:
            _flush_telemetry_for_db_path(db_path)
        except Exception:
            pass
        if conn is not None:
            conn.close_pooled()
        with _CACHE_LOCK:
            _QUERY_WRITE_COUNTS.pop(db_path, None)
            dict.pop(_INITIALIZED_DBS, db_path, None)
            extra_conn = _CONNECTION_POOL.pop(db_path, None)
            if extra_conn is not None:
                extra_conn.close_pooled()
            _evict_vector_cache_for_db_path(db_path)
        if ns:
            _evict_external_namespace_caches(ns)


def validate_memory_id(memory_id: str) -> str:
    """
    Validate a memory or contradiction identifier to prevent stored XSS, SQL/HTML injection,
    or control-character corruption.
    Allowed: 1-128 characters of ASCII letters, digits, '.', '_', ':', '-', starting with alphanumeric.
    """
    if not isinstance(memory_id, str):
        raise ValueError("Memory ID must be a string.")
    clean = memory_id.strip()
    if not clean or not _VALID_MEMORY_ID_RE.match(clean):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Invalid memory ID '{memory_id}'. Must match ^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{{0,127}}$."
        )
    return clean


# --- Mixed Embedding-Space Guards (Phase 2.1) ---


def _is_mixed_space(row_model: Optional[str], query_model: Optional[str]) -> bool:
    rm = (row_model or "").strip().lower()
    qm = (query_model or "").strip().lower()
    if not rm or not qm:
        return False
    if rm in ("unknown", "needs-reembed") or qm in ("unknown", "needs-reembed"):
        return False
    return rm != qm


def _is_same_known_space(row_model: Optional[str], query_model: Optional[str]) -> bool:
    rm = (row_model or "").strip().lower()
    qm = (query_model or "").strip().lower()
    if not rm or not qm:
        return False
    if rm in ("unknown", "needs-reembed") or qm in ("unknown", "needs-reembed"):
        return False
    return rm == qm


# --- Cross-Process + In-Process Re-Entrant Namespace Write Lock (Phase 2.2) ---


class _NamespaceLockState:
    def __init__(self, namespace: str) -> None:
        self.namespace = namespace
        self.rlock = threading.RLock()
        self.depth = 0
        self.fd: Optional[int] = None
        self.lock_path: Optional[Path] = None


_NS_LOCK_REGISTRY_MUTEX = threading.Lock()
_NS_LOCK_REGISTRY: dict[tuple[Path, str], _NamespaceLockState] = {}
_OPEN_LOCK_FDS: dict[Path, int] = {}
_OPEN_LOCK_FDS_MUTEX = threading.Lock()


def _get_ns_lock_path(namespace: str) -> Path:
    clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    ensure_secure_mesh_dir()
    base_dir = cached_resolved_mesh_dir()
    lock_path = base_dir / f".mesh_{clean_ns}.lock"
    reject_symlink(lock_path)
    return lock_path


def _acquire_os_file_lock(lock_path: Path, timeout: float) -> int:
    """
    Acquire an exclusive cross-process advisory file lock on `lock_path`.
    Reuses open file descriptors across calls to eliminate OS open/close overhead.
    """
    with _OPEN_LOCK_FDS_MUTEX:
        fd = _OPEN_LOCK_FDS.get(lock_path)
        if fd is None:
            ensure_secure_mesh_dir(lock_path.parent)
            reject_symlink(lock_path)
            flags = os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT
            flags |= getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0)
            mode = stat.S_IRUSR | stat.S_IWUSR if os.name != "nt" else 0o600
            fd = os.open(str(lock_path), flags, mode)
            if os.name != "nt":
                fchmod = getattr(os, "fchmod", None)
                if fchmod is not None:
                    try:
                        fchmod(fd, stat.S_IRUSR | stat.S_IWUSR)
                    except (AttributeError, OSError):
                        pass
            else:
                try:
                    if os.fstat(fd).st_size == 0:
                        os.write(fd, b"\x00")
                except OSError:
                    pass
            _OPEN_LOCK_FDS[lock_path] = fd

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.05, float(timeout))
    sleep_s = 0.002
    while True:
        try:
            if os.name == "nt":
                import msvcrt

                os.lseek(fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
                msvcrt.locking(fd, msvcrt.LK_NBLCK, 1)
            else:
                import fcntl

                flock = getattr(fcntl, "flock", None)
                if flock is not None:
                    lock_ex = getattr(fcntl, "LOCK_EX", 2)
                    lock_nb = getattr(fcntl, "LOCK_NB", 4)
                    flock(fd, lock_ex | lock_nb)
            return fd
        except OSError:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                raise TimeoutError(
                    f"Timed out after {timeout:.1f}s waiting for namespace write lock '{lock_path.name}'."
                )
            time.sleep(sleep_s)
            sleep_s = min(0.05, sleep_s * 1.4)


def _release_os_file_lock(fd: int, close_fd: bool = False) -> None:
    try:
        if os.name == "nt":
            import msvcrt

            try:
                os.lseek(fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
                msvcrt.locking(fd, msvcrt.LK_UNLCK, 1)
            except OSError:
                pass
        else:
            import fcntl

            flock = getattr(fcntl, "flock", None)
            if flock is not None:
                lock_un = getattr(fcntl, "LOCK_UN", 8)
                try:
                    flock(fd, lock_un)
                except OSError:
                    pass
    finally:
        if close_fd:
            try:
                os.close(fd)
            except OSError:
                pass


@contextlib.contextmanager
def namespace_write_lock(namespace: str, timeout: float = 15.0) -> Iterator[None]:
    """
    Combined per-namespace in-process `threading.RLock` + cross-process advisory file lock
    on `~/.mesh/.mesh_<ns>.lock` (mode 0600 on POSIX).
    """
    clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    mesh_dir = cached_resolved_mesh_dir()
    key = (mesh_dir, clean_ns)
    with _NS_LOCK_REGISTRY_MUTEX:
        state = _NS_LOCK_REGISTRY.get(key)
        if state is None:
            state = _NamespaceLockState(clean_ns)
            _NS_LOCK_REGISTRY[key] = state

    wait_timeout = max(0.05, float(timeout))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + wait_timeout
    if not state.rlock.acquire(timeout=wait_timeout):
        raise TimeoutError(
            f"Timed out after {timeout:.1f}s waiting for in-process namespace write lock '{clean_ns}'."
        )
    try:
        if state.depth == 0:
            remaining = max(0.05, deadline - time.monotonic())
            lock_path = _get_ns_lock_path(clean_ns)
            state.fd = _acquire_os_file_lock(lock_path, timeout=remaining)
            state.lock_path = lock_path
        state.depth += 1
        try:
            yield
        finally:
            state.depth -= 1
            if state.depth == 0 and state.fd is not None:
                fd_to_release = state.fd
                state.fd = None
                _release_os_file_lock(fd_to_release, close_fd=False)
    finally:
        state.rlock.release()


def evict_namespace_write_lock(namespace: str) -> None:
    """Evict the in-process lock registry entry for a namespace."""
    try:
        clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    except ValueError:
        return
    mesh_dir = get_mesh_dir().resolve()
    key = (mesh_dir, clean_ns)
    with _NS_LOCK_REGISTRY_MUTEX:
        state = _NS_LOCK_REGISTRY.get(key)
        if state is None:
            return
        acquired = state.rlock.acquire(timeout=0.05)
        if not acquired:
            return
        try:
            if state.depth > 0:
                return
            _NS_LOCK_REGISTRY.pop(key, None)
            if state.fd is not None:
                _release_os_file_lock(state.fd, close_fd=True)
                state.fd = None
            if state.lock_path is not None:
                with _OPEN_LOCK_FDS_MUTEX:
                    open_fd = _OPEN_LOCK_FDS.pop(state.lock_path, None)
                    if open_fd is not None:
                        try:
                            os.close(open_fd)
                        except OSError:
                            pass
        finally:
            state.rlock.release()


@atexit.register
def _cleanup_open_lock_fds() -> None:
    with _OPEN_LOCK_FDS_MUTEX:
        for fd in _OPEN_LOCK_FDS.values():
            try:
                os.close(fd)
            except OSError:
                pass
        _OPEN_LOCK_FDS.clear()



def _clean_possessives(text: str) -> str:
    s = text.lower().replace("\u2019", "'").replace("\u2018", "'").replace("`", "'")
    return re.sub(r"'s\b", "", s)


def _fast_stem(w: str) -> str:
    if len(w) <= 3:
        return w
    if w.endswith("ies") and len(w) > 4:
        return w[:-3] + "y"
    if w.endswith("ing") and len(w) > 5:
        base = w[:-3]
        return base[:-1] if len(base) >= 3 and base[-1] == base[-2] else base
    if w.endswith("ed") and len(w) > 4:
        return w[:-2]
    if w.endswith("es") and len(w) > 4:
        return w[:-2]
    if w.endswith("s") and not w.endswith("ss") and len(w) > 3:
        return w[:-1]
    return w


# --- Bidirectional Tight Domain Synonym & Compound Expansion Engine (Phase 2.5) ---
_SYNONYM_GROUPS: list[set[str]] = [
    {"color", "colour", "hue", "shade", "tint"},
    {"blue", "navy", "azure", "cyan", "cobalt", "teal", "sapphire", "indigo"},
    {"red", "crimson", "scarlet", "maroon", "ruby", "vermilion", "burgundy"},
    {"green", "emerald", "lime", "olive", "jade", "mint"},
